*Sima Hashemi,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Continually-Recyclable Unit-Gating (CRUG)，通过可微门控与单元回收机制，在持续动态系统重建中实现零遗忘并保持容量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续动态系统重建（cDSR）要求在学习新系统的同时保留旧动态，但循环模型中的微小参数更新就可能在长时间自主演化中定性改变行为，因此需要有效方法在固定容量下平衡重建能力与遗忘问题。

Method: 作者在完全可训练且可解释的 Almost-Linear RNN (AL-RNN) 上基准测试了参数正则化、回放和参数隔离等持续学习方法。随后提出 CRUG：用基于 L0 惩罚训练的可微门控选择任务特定单元，未使用单元可回收给后续任务，并通过有向连接让新任务复用旧表征而不影响已提交单元的动态。

Result: 参数隔离最能保留旧动态，但过多任务专属分配会迅速耗尽固定网络容量。CRUG 在测试方法中取得最佳的重建—容量权衡，实现零遗忘，并能可靠学习由非线性和混沌系统组成的异构序列。前向迁移在任务底层动态相似时更明显且更有用。CRUG 的优势还扩展到顺序认知任务。

Conclusion: CRUG 通过紧凑分配和前向迁移实现高效容量的持续动态系统重建，兼具零遗忘和强重建能力，并可推广到自主 cDSR 之外的顺序认知任务。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems Reconstruction (DSR) aims to infer models from observed time series that reproduce a system's qualitative long-term behavior. Continual DSR (cDSR) requires learning new systems while preserving previously learned dynamics, yet even small parameter updates in recurrent models can qualitatively alter their behavior over long autonomous rollouts. We benchmark established continual learning (CL) methods spanning parameter regularization, replay, and parameter isolation on the fully trainable and interpretable Almost-Linear RNN (AL-RNN). Parameter isolation preserves earlier dynamics most effectively, but excessive task-specific allocations can rapidly exhaust a fixed-size network. We therefore introduce Continually-Recyclable Unit-Gating (CRUG), which conserves capacity through compact allocation and forward transfer. Differentiable gates trained with an $L_0$-based penalty select task-specific units, while unused units are recycled for subsequent tasks. Directed connections allow later tasks to reuse earlier representations without affecting the dynamics of previously committed units. CRUG achieves the strongest reconstruction--capacity trade-off among the tested methods with zero forgetting and reliably learns a heterogeneous sequence of nonlinear and chaotic systems. Furthermore, we show that forward transfer is more pronounced and useful when tasks share similar underlying dynamics. Lastly, we demonstrate that CRUG's advantages extend beyond autonomous cDSR to sequential cognitive tasks.

</details>


### [51] [On the Off-Policy Teacher in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38360)
*Langlin Huang,Hao Liu,Mononito Goswami,Xinyu Li,Prithwith Jana,Nikos Kanakaris,Patrick Blöbaum,Purak Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCOUT 框架，通过共生训练让学生前缀的教师条件能力得到提升，从而改进在线策略蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，学生从自身策略生成的轨迹中学习，并由教师提供密集监督。但存在根本性不对称：虽然采样轨迹对学生是在线策略的，但对教师却是离线策略的——教师原本基于自身策略生成的前缀优化，却在 OPD 中不得不监督学生生成的前缀，其续写能力随前缀变长而下降。

Method: 提出 SCOUT（Student-COnditioned Updates of the Teacher）共生训练框架。在标准 OPD 更新之外，SCOUT 定期用可验证奖励的强化学习优化教师的条件能力：教师从学生前缀生成续写，并从结果奖励中学习，从而适配学生生成的前缀。

Result: 受控实验表明 SCOUT 提升了教师从学生生成前缀继续写作的能力，验证了学生条件化教师适配的预期机制。在多种教师-学生配置、模型规模和推理领域上，SCOUT 也持续提升了在线策略蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: SCOUT 通过协同优化教师以适配学生前缀，缓解了 OPD 中教师离线策略的不对称问题，提升了蒸馏效果，具有跨配置和领域的通用性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a promising post-training paradigm in which the student learns from trajectories generated by its own policy under dense teacher supervision. However, OPD introduces a fundamental asymmetry: although the sampled trajectories are on-policy for the student, they are off-policy for the teacher. The teacher is typically optimized to continue from prefixes generated by its own policy, but during OPD it must instead supervise prefixes generated by the student. Empirically, we find that its continuation performance degrades as these prefixes grow longer. To address this issue, we propose Student-COnditioned Updates of the Teacher (SCOUT), a co-training framework that adapts the teacher to student-generated prefixes. Alongside standard OPD updates, SCOUT periodically optimizes the teacher's conditional ability using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where the teacher generates continuations from student prefixes and learns from outcome rewards. Controlled experiments show that SCOUT improves the teacher's ability to continue from student-generated prefixes, supporting the intended mechanism of student-conditioned teacher adaptation. Across multiple teacher--student configurations, model scales, and reasoning domains, SCOUT also consistently improves the effectiveness of on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [52] [Simulator-Refined Diffusion for Radio-Frequency Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38363)
*Jinhao Liang,Jacob K. Christopher,Michael Frei,Tommaso Dreossi,Nando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD)，将低保真可微代理模型与高保真不可微仿真器结合到扩散采样中，实现满足电磁指标的 PCB 布局逆设计，性能超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在 PCB 逆设计中具有潜力，但难以满足定量电磁指标。基于梯度的引导常用，但全波电磁仿真器精确却昂贵且不可微，可微代理虽能提供梯度信息却不够可靠。

Method: 提出 SRD 方法：在扩散采样过程中结合低保真可微代理与高保真不可微仿真器。不同于需要大量随机扰动的零阶优化，该方法利用代理梯度提出扰动方向，再由仿真器沿该方向搜索，确定有效的设计更新。

Result: 在不同设置下的实验表明，该方法持续优于当前最先进方法，生成的布局其仿真 S 参数与目标规格的匹配度在同分布目标上提升达 21.2%，在分布外目标上提升达 19.8%。

Conclusion: 通过将代理梯度与仿真器搜索结合，SRD 有效缓解了梯度引导中仿真器不可微与代理不可靠的矛盾，提升了 PCB 布局逆设计的电磁指标满足度，并展现出对分布外目标良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown potential in inverse design of printed circuit boards (PCBs), enabling the generation of layouts conditioned on target S-parameters. Despite this promise, applying diffusion models to PCB layout generation remains challenging due to their difficulty in meeting the quantitative electromagnetic specifications. A common approach is gradient-based guidance, which biases the diffusion sampling process with the gradient of an objective used for evaluation. However, full-wave electromagnetic simulators are accurate but expensive and typically non-differentiable, whereas differentiable surrogates are informative but not always reliable. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD), a novel combination of a low-fidelity differentiable surrogate and a high-fidelity non-differentiable simulator within the diffusion sampling process. Unlike standard zeroth-order optimization, which requires a great number of random perturbations, our approach uses the surrogate's gradient to propose the perturbation direction while the simulator then searches based on this direction to identify an effective design update. Experimental results across different settings show that this method consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art methods, producing layouts whose simulated S-parameters match the target specifications up to 21.2% closer for in-distribution targets and up to 19.8% for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [53] [Learning to Plan from Random Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38383)
*Deqian Kong,Guangyan Sun,Sheng Cheng,Sirui Xie,Bo Pang,Jianwen Xie,Tony Geng,Caiwen Ding,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用随机游走产生的数据学习多尺度时间关系，并以此在无策略改进训练的情况下完成长程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索能在目标未指定前揭示环境的可通行结构，但能否仅凭这种经验支持长程规划而不需要策略改进训练，仍不明确。作者希望明确时间关系所包含的地理与连通性信息，并利用它做规划。

Method: 通过随机游走分析时间关系：短时域在扩散极限下反映测地几何，长时域在混合前反映区域连通性。用条件能量模型，经噪声对比估计在观测对上训练，估计时域条件下的时间对数密度比，无需动作或奖励标签。规划器在不同时域查询这些关系，测试时用独立的局部动力学模型预测候选动作结果，时间模型评估其对目标的进展，执行一个动作后重规划，两个模型固定。

Result: 在迷宫长程规划任务中，仅用随机探索的 states 和 images 即取得成功。学习的 score field、embedding 探针和规划路径表现出多尺度认知地图的特性；此外还展示了从随机探索进行自我中心导航，以及从次优数据做操作规划。

Conclusion: 随机探索数据中的时间关系足以支持长程规划，无需策略改进训练，且所学表征呈现多尺度认知地图性质，可推广到导航与操作规划。

Abstract: Random exploration reveals how an environment can be traversed before a goal is specified. Can this experience support long-range planning without policy-improvement training? Our random-walk analysis explains what temporal relations contain: short horizons reveal geodesic geometry in the diffusion limit, while longer horizons reveal connectivity between regions before mixing removes these distinctions. We learn these relations with a conditional energy-based model that estimates temporal log-density ratios through horizon-conditioned embeddings. The model is trained on observation pairs by noise-contrastive estimation, without action or reward labels. The planner queries these learned relations at different horizons as it moves toward the goal. At test time, a separate local dynamics model predicts candidate action outcomes, and the temporal model evaluates their progress toward the goal by selecting or aggregating estimated improvements across horizons. The agent executes one action and replans with both models fixed. Experiments demonstrate long-range maze planning from random exploration using states and images. Learned score fields, embedding probes, and planned routes exhibit properties of a multiscale cognitive map. We further demonstrate egocentric navigation from random exploration and manipulation planning from suboptimal data.

</details>


### [54] [LeanPolish: Verified Supervision for Lean Proof Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38384)
*Pauline Bourigault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanPolish 是一个符号化 Lean 4 流水线，发布 33,402 条被接受的局部编辑与 65,596 条同状态失败尝试，用于区分"模仿搜索策略"与"真正改进搜索"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已验证的证明编辑是改进语言模型生成 Lean 证明的天然监督来源，但验证只能说明编辑正确，不能说明训练信号中没有搜索伪影（如排序捷径、平凡删除）。需要可控评估来分离正确性、压缩与编辑策略。

Method: 构建符号化 Lean 4 流水线 LeanPolish，释放接受的局部编辑与同状态失败尝试数据；通过"首次成功"与"延续菜单评估"对比研究排序捷径；训练排序器；对压缩任务迭代符号化流程并与神经混合方法对比；设置冻结模型对照，并微调进行整体证明重写。

Result: 首次成功搜索存在目标无关规则可达完美排序准确率，且教师选择的评估点奖励平凡删除；延续菜单评估消除排序捷径后，训练排序器在 70.1% 的留出状态上选中最优候选，最强冻结基线仅 36.9%。压缩方面，迭代符号化流程将 miniF2F 节省率从 19.7% 提升到 27.5%，超过所测神经混合方法。已验证的神经编辑在其他证明来源上有效，但匹配的冻结模型对照表明其增益未必来自训练。整体证明重写方面，微调将 19 个 PutnamBench 证明的已验证 token 缩减从 2.8% 提升到 5.5%。

Conclusion: 发布的编辑、完整候选池与受控评估区分了"学习模仿搜索策略"与"改进搜索"，为在保持正确性、压缩和编辑策略相互独立的前提下研究证明改进提供了可复现基础。

Abstract: Verified proof edits offer a natural source of supervision for improving language-model-generated Lean proofs. Yet verification establishes that an edit is correct, not that its training signal is free of search artifacts. We introduce LeanPolish, a symbolic Lean 4 pipeline that releases 33,402 accepted local edits and 65,596 same-state failed attempts, and use it to study what models learn from this supervision. First-success search admits a goal-independent rule with perfect ranking accuracy; teacher-selected evaluation sites also reward trivial deletions. Continuing menu evaluation beyond the first success removes the ordering shortcut: a trained ranker selects the best candidate on 70.1% of evaluated held-out states, versus 36.9% for the strongest frozen baseline. For compression, iterating the symbolic pass raises miniF2F savings from 19.7% to 27.5%, exceeding the neural hybrids we test there. Verified neural editing helps on other proof sources, but matched frozen-model controls show that its gains need not come from training. The supervision does improve whole-proof rewriting: fine-tuning raises verified token reduction from 2.8% to 5.5% on 19 PutnamBench proofs. Together, the released edits, complete candidate pools, and controlled evaluations separate learning to imitate a search policy from improving on that search. They provide a reproducible basis for studying proof improvement while keeping correctness, compression, and edit policy distinct.

</details>


### [55] [Which Tasks Survive Self-Supervised Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38393)
*Achleshwar Luthra,Lucas Bryant,Tracy Zhu,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究同实例自监督学习（SSL）的表征能保留哪些下游任务信息，提出"语义可恢复性"概念：任务后验被表征函数空间捕获的程度。对中心化白化表征，可恢复性精确决定方向性CDNV、控制少样本最近质心分类；对典型双视图SSL目标，其总体最优解张成双视图算子的前导跨视图稳定模态，从而给出可恢复性的闭式谱刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同实例SSL仅通过强制同一实例两个视图的一致性来学习表征，但这一原则本身并不能决定哪些下游任务可从学到的表征中恢复。作者希望从任务层面回答：什么信息在SSL后存活，以及这些保留信息如何体现在下游几何与迁移中。

Method: 1. 定义"语义可恢复性"为任务后验分数被表征函数空间捕获的量。2. 对中心化、白化表征，推导可恢复性与方向类距离归一化方差（CDNV）、少样本最近质心分类、任务相关语义方向强度的关系。3. 分析典型双视图SSL目标的总体最优解，证明其张成双视图算子的前导跨视图稳定模态。4. 由此得到语义可恢复性的闭式谱刻画，并在合成与真实数据集上跨多种SSL方法进行验证，检验可恢复性、方向几何、谱结构与少样本迁移之间的预测关系。

Result: 理论结果表明：可恢复性精确决定方向性CDNV，控制少样本最近质心分类，并支配任务相关语义方向的强度；总体线性探针与质心轴重合，多个被良好恢复的任务趋于因子化质心几何。下游任务被保留的程度取决于其后验是否落在选定的谱子空间内。合成与真实数据实验验证了可恢复性、方向几何、谱结构和少样本迁移之间的预测关系。

Conclusion: 研究为同实例SSL提供了任务层面的解释：说明了哪些信息能在SSL中存活，以及保留的信息如何体现在下游几何与迁移性能中。可恢复性的谱刻画为理解SSL表征的任务可迁移性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Same-instance self-supervised learning (SSL) learns representations by enforcing consistency across two views of the same underlying instance. This principle alone, however, does not determine which downstream tasks remain recoverable from the learned representation. We study this question through \emph{semantic recoverability}, defined as the amount of a task's posterior score captured by the represented function space. We show that, for centered and whitened representations, recoverability exactly determines directional class-distance-normalized variance (CDNV), controls few-shot nearest-centroid classification, and governs the strength of task-relevant semantic directions. The population linear probe and centroid axis coincide, and multiple well-recovered tasks approach a factorial centroid geometry. We then analyze a canonical two-view SSL objective and show that its population optimum spans the leading cross-view-stable modes of the associated two-view operator. This yields a closed-form spectral characterization of semantic recoverability: a downstream task is preserved to the extent that its posterior lies in the selected spectral subspace. We validate these predictions on synthetic and real datasets across several SSL methods, testing the predicted relationships among recoverability, directional geometry, spectral structure, and few-shot transfer. Together, these results give a task-level account of what information survives same-instance SSL and how the retained information appears in downstream geometry and transfer.

</details>


### [56] [Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning for Image-Based Continuous Control with Gaussian and Beta Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38407)
*Irving Giovani Bronzatti Petrazzini,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过CarRacing控制实验评估DRIL（分歧正则化模仿学习）在图像连续控制中的效果，发现其在少量示范场景下显著优于行为克隆，但在示范较多时优势缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在学习控制器访问示范分布之外的状态时会累积误差，亟需方法缓解该问题，因此本研究评估将克隆策略间分歧转化为强化学习奖励的DRIL方法在图像连续控制中的改进效果。

Method: 采用受控的CarRacing研究，组合高斯与Beta学习策略、来自裁剪高斯专家或有界Beta专家的示范、1条或20条轨迹、确定性与随机性评估，并保留行为克隆、最高10回合训练分数检查点及最终DRIL检查点三个阶段；每个变体中分歧集成都包含五个高斯策略，并对每个保留策略在100个程序生成回合上评估。

Result: 分数选择的DRIL在少量示范情形增益最大：相较最强行为克隆均值，裁剪动作示范提升61%，有界动作示范提升112%。在20条轨迹时DRIL优势收窄；在有界动作机制下，Beta行为克隆仍比最佳DRIL检查点高约7%。分歧奖励的信息量随学习器表示与训练阶段而变化。

Conclusion: DRIL能显著改进少量示范的视觉连续控制，而示范更多时具有界Beta策略的行为克隆表现强劲，结果凸显学习器支撑、集成响应与检查点选择的共同重要性。

Abstract: Purpose: Behavior cloning can accumulate errors when a learned controller visits states outside the demonstrated distribution. This study evaluates whether Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning (DRIL), which converts disagreement among cloned policies into a reinforcement-learning reward, improves image-based continuous control. Methods: A controlled CarRacing study combines Gaussian and Beta learner policies, demonstrations from either a clipped Gaussian expert or an intrinsically bounded Beta expert, one or 20 trajectories, deterministic and stochastic evaluation, and three retained stages: behavior cloning, the highest 10-episode training-score checkpoint, and the final DRIL checkpoint. The disagreement ensemble contains five Gaussian policies in every variant. Each retained policy is evaluated over 100 procedurally generated episodes. Results: Score-selected DRIL produced its largest gains in the few-demonstration setting, improving over the strongest behavior-cloning mean by 61% with clipped-action demonstrations and by 112% with bounded-action demonstrations. With 20 trajectories, the advantage of DRIL narrowed; in the bounded-action regime, Beta behavior cloning remained about 7% above the best DRIL checkpoint. The experiments also show that the informativeness of the disagreement reward changes with the learner representation and training stage. Conclusion: DRIL can substantially improve few-demonstration visual continuous control, while bounded Beta policies provide strong behavior-cloning performance when more demonstrations are available. The results highlight the joint importance of learner support,ensemble response, and checkpoint selection.

</details>


### [57] [Synthesis Without Training: An Inference-Only Pipeline for Tabular, Temporal, and Relational Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38414)
*Zilong Zhao,Abdul Raheem,Jiayu Li,Sohei Arisaka,Darius Lim Hong Yi,Milad Abdollahzadeh,Uzair Javaid,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GENSCRIPT，一种免训练的推理式合成数据生成管线，通过统计画像+语言模型+编码代理生成可执行采样器，支持单表、时序、关系数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"先拟合后采样"范式存在三大问题：每个数据集都需重新训练；不同模态需专用模型和特征工程；模型不透明，难以在数据约束下检查行为。

Method: GENSCRIPT先计算源数据的确定性统计画像（列类型、范围、缺失、类别、相关性等），将画像而非原始行传给语言模型以推断字段语义和跨列完整性约束，再由编码代理将画像与约束编译为可执行、可审计的采样器。

Result: 四个单表基准上，2分钟构建生成器、6秒采样5万行，边缘保真度与领先方法仅差几个点；是唯一完美保持Adult数据集列间1对1映射的方法。智能建筑数据集上生成的条件时序比两个基线更接近真实分布，并在相应关系数据库中完美保持主外键关系。

Conclusion: GENSCRIPT以统一、无需训练的方式支持单表、时序和关系数据生成，兼具高效性、可审计性与跨模态通用性。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation is dominated by the fit-then-sample paradigm: a generative model is trained on a private dataset and then sampled from. Despite its widespread adoption, this paradigm faces three challenges: (1) a new training run is required for every dataset; (2) different data modalities, such as single tables, time series, and relational databases, require task-specific models and feature engineering; and (3) the resulting model is opaque, making its behavior under data constraints difficult to inspect. We propose GENSCRIPT, an inference-only pipeline that eliminates model training. GENSCRIPT computes a deterministic statistical profile of the source data (column types, ranges, missingness, categories, correlations, etc.) and passes it--rather than raw rows--to a language model to infer field semantics and cross-column integrity constraints. A coding agent then compiles the profile and constraints into an executable, auditable sampler. This unified approach supports single-table, temporal, and relational data without task-specific modeling. Across four single-table benchmarks, GENSCRIPT builds generators in 2 minutes and samples 50k rows within 6 seconds, while remaining within a few points of leading methods in marginal fidelity. Notably, it is the only method that perfectly preserves a 1-to-1 mapping between columns in the Adult dataset. On a smart-building dataset, it produces conditional time series that more closely match the real distribution than two baselines and perfectly preserves primary- and foreign-key relationships in the corresponding relational database.

</details>


### [58] [Graph Anomaly Detection as Finite-Horizon Control: Training-Free Scoring via Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38424)
*Fred Xu,Thomas Markovich,Florence Regol,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EB-GAD：一种免训练的经验贝叶斯图异常检测框架，将正常性建模为图感知广义OU弛豫，把打分转化为闭式有限时域控制能量，在11个基准的9个上取得最佳/并列最佳AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节点级图异常检测（GAD）通过特定架构、消息传递、重构或对比目标间接编码正常性与异常分数，把图信任（结构在多大程度上定义正常性）、图谱加权和异常分数选择纠缠在一起，导致分数昂贵、不透明且在不同图模式下不稳定。因此需要一个将三者解耦、无需训练的方法。

Method: 提出EB-GAD（Empirical-Bayes GAD），一种免训练框架：用图感知的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（GOU）弛豫过程向图滤波模板收敛来建模正常性；用经验贝叶斯从残差场似然中拟合图精度；GOU使打分成为闭式有限时域控制能量（沿图谱弛豫将特征中性节点引导至观测端点的最小代价）。扫描弛豫时域与端点容差得到共享同一拟合先验的分数族：平衡态Mahalanobis打分是其一个极限，有限时域控制能量与尺度归一化比值分数可揭示静态平衡打分掩盖的异常。再由无标签选择器依据特征同质性、边密度与特征维度选择分数族，并按拟合零假设偏离与排序稳定性对候选排序。

Result: 在11个基准、全流程不使用标签的情况下，EB-GAD在9个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳AUROC：四个金融欺诈网络（最大达370万节点）、YelpChi与Amazon评论图、Weibo、Reddit与Facebook，优势最高达21.7个百分点；在BlogCatalog与ACM上排名第二。

Conclusion: 将图信任、谱加权与分数选择从学习架构中解耦，并作为图感知弛豫的先验拟合问题来处理，可在无需标签、无需训练的情况下获得更强、更稳定且可解释的图异常检测效果。

Abstract: Node-level graph anomaly detection (GAD) identifies nodes whose attributes and interactions deviate from dominant graph regularities. Existing GAD models encode normality and anomaly scoring indirectly through architectures, message passing, reconstruction or contrastive objectives, and tuned score families. This entangles graph trust (how strongly graph structure should define normality), graph-spectral weighting, and anomaly-score choice, yielding scores that are costly, opaque, and unstable across graph regimes. We propose EB-GAD (Empirical-Bayes GAD), a training-free framework that models normality as graph-aware generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (GOU) relaxation toward a graph-filtered template. Empirical Bayes fits the graph precision from the residual-field likelihood; the GOU then turns scoring into a closed-form finite-horizon control energy, the minimum effort to steer a feature-neutral node to its observed endpoint along graph-spectral relaxation. Sweeping relaxation horizon and endpoint tolerance yields a bank of scores that share one fitted prior: equilibrium Mahalanobis scoring is one limit, while finite-horizon control-energy and scale-normalized ratio scores reveal anomalies that static equilibrium scoring can mask. A label-free selector chooses the score family from feature homophily, edge density, and feature dimension, then ranks candidates by fitted-null deviation and rank stability. On 11 benchmarks and without labels at any step, EB-GAD has the best or tied-best AUROC on 9: the four financial fraud networks (up to 3.7M nodes), the YelpChi and Amazon review graphs, Weibo, Reddit and Facebook, with margins of up to 21.7 points. It is second on BlogCatalog and ACM.

</details>


### [59] [What Pretraining and Midtraining Make Learnable from Rewards?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38446)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究预训练与中期训练如何为奖励适配提供信息与计算基础，区分了"预测能力"（执行或检索）与"奖励学习任务特定用法"两部分，并用理论与实验证明其分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励信号只能标识正确答案，却无法确定新输入所需的计算过程；因此在序列状态计算与上下文记忆任务中，存在多种机制在训练奖励上完全一致、但在保留测试集上给出不同答案，需要明确预训练/中期训练提供何种信息与计算来消除这种歧义。

Method: 作者在序列状态计算与上下文记忆场景中刻画了训练奖励相同但保留答案不同的机制，并指出与任务无关的源观测可消除歧义；随后构造从指定随机初始化出发、在同一参数上依次经过源预测与奖励适配的有限采样 Adam 路径，证明预测如何获得执行/检索能力、奖励如何学到任务特定用法；最后用预训练 Qwen2.5 检查点在八个世界及 GSM8K、HotpotQA 上验证该分工。

Result: 八个世界中，使用正确源与首操作监督的 Sequential 模型达到 82.61% 成功率，而私有随机源对照仅 44.15%；记忆回放可在奖励适配中保留检索能力，独立八世界验证达 75.32%，匹配的替代检索训练仅 49.86%；GSM8K 与 HotpotQA 将奖励进入时的准确率、后续增益与最终表现分离开来。

Conclusion: 研究结果将信息获取、可执行计算与奖励引导的任务学习联系起来，表明奖励适配的有效性依赖于预训练/中期训练阶段已建立的计算与信息结构，而非仅靠奖励本身。

Abstract: A reward can identify a correct answer while leaving the computation needed for new inputs undetermined. We study how pretraining and midtraining supply the information and computation that make reward adaptation effective. In sequential state computation and contextual memory, we characterize mechanisms that agree on every training reward yet demand different held-out answers. Task-independent source observations resolve this ambiguity. We construct finite sampled Adam paths from specified random initializations through source prediction and reward adaptation in the same parameters, proving how prediction acquires execution or retrieval and rewards learn their task-specific use. Experiments with pretrained Qwen2.5 checkpoints test this division of labor. Across eight worlds, Sequential models trained with correct source and first-operation supervision reach 82.61% success, versus 44.15% for a private-random source control. Memory replay preserves retrieval during reward adaptation, and an independent eight-world confirmation achieves 75.32% task success versus 49.86% after matched alternative-retrieval training. GSM8K and HotpotQA separate accuracy at reward entry, subsequent gain and final performance. Together, these results connect information acquisition, executable computation and reward-guided task learning.

</details>


### [60] [An Input-Frugal Deep Learning Framework for Weather-Driven National Crop-Yield Forecasting: A Case Study of Brazilian Soybean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38447)
*Fernando Dupin da Cunha Mello,Prashant Kumar,Erick G. Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种节省、可迁移、架构无关的深度学习框架，仅用常规天气数据加两个轻量静态输入预测作物产量，在巴西大豆案例中Transformer表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠、及时的作物产量预测对市场稳定和风险管理至关重要，但许多现有方法依赖成本高或难以规模化的输入数据，因此需要一个精简、可迁移的预测框架。

Method: 以常规天气为唯一时变输入，加上作物年份和农业环境标签两个轻量静态输入；在相同数据要求下支持多种序列编码器（MLP、CNN、LSTM、CNN-LSTM、Transformer编码器、Mamba状态空间模型），对比线性岭回归和五年移动平均基线。采用巴西大豆2001/02-2020/21共20个生长季数据和留一年交叉验证。

Result: 所有深度学习变体优于岭回归，序列编码器均超过非序列MLP；Transformer全国精度最佳（RMSE 149 kg/ha，rRMSE 5.3%，R²=0.784），较农户基线误差降低47.6%；季内预测从早季到晚季误差单调下降，最新预测点误差比基线低约50%。消融显示农业环境标签和空间实例扩展有正向贡献且不增加输入复杂度；SHAP诊断表明作物年份主导长期趋势，季内天气和农业环境背景主导年际偏差，其中湿度/云量和热量需求变量影响最大。

Conclusion: 该框架易于在其他作物和地理区域部署，并天然兼容业务化天气预报以支持常规监测。

Abstract: Reliable, timely crop-yield forecasts are essential for market stability and risk management, yet many approaches rely on costly or hard-to-scale inputs. We present a frugal, transferable, and architecture-agnostic deep learning framework that uses routine weather as the only time-varying input plus two lightweight static context inputs (crop year and an agro-environmental label) to capture long-run change and regional heterogeneity, while supporting multiple sequence encoders under identical data requirements. Using a 20-season Brazilian soybean case study (2001/02-2020/21) with leave-one-year-out cross-validation, we benchmark MLP, CNN, LSTM, CNN-LSTM, a Transformer encoder and the Mamba state-space model against linear ridge regression and a five-year moving-average "farmer" baseline. All deep learning variants outperform ridge, and all sequential encoders surpass the non-sequential MLP. The Transformer achieves the best national accuracy (RMSE 149 kg ha^-1; rRMSE 5.3%; R^2 = 0.784), reducing error by 47.6% relative to the farmer baseline. In-season forecasts improve monotonically from early- to late-season issuance, reaching approximately 50% lower error than the baseline at the latest forecast point. Ablations indicate that the agro-environmental label and spatial instance expansion (multiple grid-node weather sequences per municipality-year) contribute positively without increasing input complexity. SHAP diagnostics suggest crop year explains most of the long-run trajectory, whereas within-season weather and agro-environmental context primarily drive interannual deviations, with moisture/cloud and thermal-demand variables dominating. Overall, the framework is straightforward to deploy across other crops and geographic regions and is naturally compatible with operational weather forecasts for routine monitoring.

</details>


### [61] [Grokking through the Lens of Minimum-Norm Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38453)
*Gil Kur,Ileana Rugina,Clémentine Carla Juliette Dominé,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立统计理论，刻画正则化几何与信号稀疏性如何控制插值附近的泛化，解释了延迟泛化（grokking）的定量机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对grokking（延迟泛化）如何依赖归纳偏置和信号结构的定量刻画不足，本文旨在填补这一空白，从统计理论角度研究正则化几何和信号稀疏性对插值附近泛化的影响。

Method: 聚焦高维回归的原型设定，构造一族凸范数，刻画稀疏促进正则在强过参数化无噪声问题下的零-一泛化律；在特征维数与样本量成比例时，精确刻画沿ℓ_r正则化路径的训练和泛化误差。

Result: 证明稀疏促进正则可使精确插值比近似拟合更准确；发现泛化增益随范数更促进稀疏、目标更稀疏而增大，在无噪声数据和ℓ_1正则时泛化出现骤降；在对角线性网络和模算术Transformer上验证理论；揭示最小范数插值的统计不稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文定量刻画了正则化几何和稀疏性对插值附近泛化的控制，为grokking提供统计解释，并揭示最小范数插值对正则强度扰动的敏感性。

Abstract: Grokking shows that fitting the training data and learning the underlying signal can occur at very different stages. However, existing theories offer limited quantitative insight into how this delayed generalization depends on inductive bias and signal structure. Our work addresses the gap by developing a statistical theory that characterizes how regularization geometry and signal sparsity govern generalization near interpolation. In particular, we focus on the prototypical setting of high-dimensional regression and identify regimes in which sparsity-promoting regularization makes exact interpolation much more accurate than approximate fitting. In strongly overparameterized noiseless problems, we prove a zero--one generalization law and construct a family of convex norms whose interpolators transition from the trivial risk of the all-zero predictor to exact recovery, while keeping the training error equal to $0$. Furthermore, when feature dimension and sample size are proportional, we provide a precise characterization of training and generalization errors along $\ell_r$-regularization paths. This in turn allows us to quantify the generalization gain that remains near interpolation: we show that this gain increases as the norm becomes more sparsity-promoting and as the target becomes sparser, with a sharp drop in generalization reached for noiseless data and $\ell_1$ regularization. Experiments on diagonal linear networks and transformers trained on modular arithmetic demonstrate the generality of our theoretical predictions. Finally, beyond grokking, our work reveals a statistical instability in minimum-norm interpolation: small perturbations in the regularization strength can lead to drastically different generalization, while preserving small training error.

</details>


### [62] [Does Gradient Conflict Predict the Understanding--Generation Trade-off? A Controlled Audit of Conflict-Metric Validity in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38465)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控实验平台GRIDUMM直接检验了"梯度冲突是理解-生成权衡的可靠诊断指标"这一假设，发现方向性冲突指标与最终权衡几乎无相关且无因果影响，而功能干扰指标和训练损失表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型日益围绕理解与生成目标之间的梯度冲突来设计，但"降低这些冲突指标能改善下游理解-生成权衡"这一前提从未被直接检验。论文旨在审计这一被广泛默认的假设是否成立。

Method: 构建受控测试平台GRIDUMM，镜像统一多模态模型训练的关键结构要素，同时使真实的权衡可被精确计算。在63种配置、372个测量检查点上，检验方向性冲突指标与最终权衡之间的Spearman相关性，并通过剂量-反应干预来分离相关性与因果性。

Result: 训练期间的任何方向性冲突指标与最终权衡的绝对Spearman相关系数均未达到0.3且置信区间不包含零。单调抑制冲突的剂量-反应干预后，权衡保持平坦，说明相关不等于因果。范数比是生成失败的检测器，在已掌握生成的配置中失效；功能干扰指标优于方向性冲突指标，而训练损失与权衡的关联很强。

Conclusion: 结果并非表明冲突无用，而是说明冲突作为诊断目标的有效性必须被确立而非假定。论文发布了审计协议作为可复用的标准。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) are increasingly designed around gradient conflict between understanding and generation objectives. The premise that reducing these metrics improves the downstream understanding-generation trade-off has never been tested directly. We audit it in a controlled testbed, GRIDUMM, which mirrors key structural ingredients of UMM training while making the ground-truth trade-off exactly computable. Across 63 configurations and 372 measured checkpoints, no directional conflict metric reaches an absolute Spearman correlation of 0.3 with a confidence interval excluding zero for conflict measured during training against the eventual trade-off. A dose-response intervention that monotonically suppresses conflict leaves the trade-off flat, separating correlation from causation. The norm ratio is a generation-failure detector and becomes null among configurations that master generation. Functional interference measures outperform directional conflict metrics, while training loss tracks the trade-off strongly. Our results do not show that conflict is useless; they show that its validity as a diagnostic target must be established, not assumed, and we release the audit protocol as a reusable standard.

</details>


### [63] [RetroGEF: Dynamic Graph Edit Flow for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38484)
*Xiaozhuang Song,Xuemin Chen,Xinjian Zhao,Yaoyao Xu,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RetroGEF，一种基于流的单步逆合成生成模型，无需固定图大小即可在同一生成过程中建模分子图变换与图规模变化，在基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆合成在药物发现与材料设计中至关重要。其涉及可同时改变连接性和图大小的分子图变换，并可能引入目标分子中不存在的反应物组分。已有方法常依赖固定大小的图画布或预设编辑顺序，难以自然建模这些变换。

Method: 提出RetroGEF，基于流的生成模型用于单步逆合成。以目标分子为起点，通过添加原子和改变分子图中的化学键来构建可能的反应物；在同一生成过程中建模分子变换与图规模变化，而非依赖固定尺寸图；直接从产物—反应物对学习，无需规定编辑顺序。

Result: 在具有代表性的逆合成基准实验上，RetroGEF取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: RetroGEF通过流式生成在统一过程中建模分子图变换与规模变化，无需固定图画布和预设编辑顺序，为单步逆合成提供了有效且性能领先的方案。

Abstract: Retrosynthesis enables the discovery of viable synthetic routes to target molecules. It plays a central role in modern drug discovery and materials design. Retrosynthesis involves molecular graph transformations that can change both connectivity and graph size. These transformations may introduce reactant components absent from the target while revising the product-derived structure. To model these transformations, we propose RetroGEF, a flow-based generative model for single-step retrosynthesis. Starting from the target molecule, it constructs possible reactants by adding atoms and changing bonds in the molecular graph. RetroGEF models molecular transformations and changes in graph size within the same generative process, rather than relying on a fixed-size graph canvas. It learns this process directly from product--reactant pairs without requiring a prescribed edit order. Experiments on representative retrosynthesis benchmarks demonstrate that RetroGEF achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [64] [Role-guided Speaker Deletion Verification in Clinical Psychiatry Speech Recordings with Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38491)
*Joseph T Colonel,Daniel Katzman,Kelsey Kirker,Adam N Davidson,Shalaila S Haas,Cheryl Corcoran,René S Kahn,Guillermo Checci,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对精神科临床对话录音中"指定说话人删除验证"问题，构建了两条对称流水线，分别验证临床医生语音与患者语音是否已从录音中移除，并用音频-语言模型与大语言模型对删除后音频进行扫描检测漏删。在48段双人精神科录音上仅推理测试了四个开放权重模型，十四个"模型-视图"配置的OR集成达到F1 0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），优于单模型，主要收益来自召回提升，表明模型与上下文视图之间存在显著互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神科临床研究日益依赖大规模口语数据以识别声学与语言学标志物。但不断演变的知情同意与协议要求，可能迫使研究者在多说话人录音中删除指定说话人，并需在整个人语料库规模上验证删除是否彻底——人工审核在规模上不可行。因此需要自动化的删除验证方法。

Method: 以角色驱动的精神科双人临床对话为对象，设计两条并行且对称的流水线：一条确认临床医生语音已从访谈录音中移除，另一条确认患者语音已从同一批录音中移除。每条流水线先对目标角色的原始音频进行脱除（redact），再用音频-语言模型与大语言模型扫描剩余输出，识别遗漏的删除。数据为48段精神科双人录音；仅推理设置下测试四个开放权重模型：Gemma-4-12B、Gemma-4-31B、Nemotron-3-Nano、Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni。

Result: 在14个"模型-视图"配置上构建的析取OR集成取得综合F1为0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），优于各单模型估计；性能提升主要来自召回增益，说明不同模型与不同上下文视图之间存在显著的互补性。

Conclusion: 多模型、多上下文视图的OR集成可用于大规模验证临床录音中指定说话人语音是否已被有效移除，其价值主要体现在高召回（漏删检出）而非精确率。当前精确率仍偏低，提示该验证流程可在减少人工审核负担方面提供初步支持，但离实用部署仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Clinical research in psychiatry increasingly relies on large scale collection of spoken language data to identify acoustic and linguistic biomarkers. Yet evolving consent and protocol requirements can oblige investigators to remove a designated speaker from multi-speaker recordings and to verify said removal at a scale infeasible for manual review of entire corpora. We study this verification problem for role-driven dyadic clinical dialogue in psychiatry and investigate it with two parallel, symmetric pipelines: confirming that clinician speech has been removed from psychiatric interview recordings, and confirming that patient speech has been removed from the same recordings. Each pipeline redacts the raw audio for its target role and then scans the surviving output with audio-language and large-language models to identify missed deletions. We evaluate this approach on a corpus of 48 dyadic recordings drawn from psychiatry settings, testing four open-weight models in an inference-only setting: Gemma-4-12B, Gemma-4-31B, Nemotron-3-Nano, and Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni. A disjunctive OR ensemble over fourteen model-view configurations had a combined F1 of 0.478 (precision 0.330, recall 0.870), an improvement over individual model estimates driven by recall gains that point to substantial complementarity across models and context views.

</details>


### [65] [Autoregressive Frontier Expansion: Growing Trees with Graph Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38506)
*Umer Gupta,Saku Peltonen,Martin Ritzert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Autoregressive Frontier Expansion，一个模拟生物生长过程的树状结构生成框架，通过流匹配模型与 SO(2)-等变图神经网络迭代扩展前沿，在皮层神经元与植物树的无条件、类别条件及形态引导生成任务上，生成形态与参考分布高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状分支结构（如植物树、神经元、血管、呼吸树）的分支形态往往反映其功能，因此结构建模对理解这些系统至关重要。由于获取真实三维数据成本高或不可行，逼真的生成模型对仿真与数据增强具有重要价值。现有方法存在局限：针对特定形态的模型要么限制拓扑生成方式，要么依赖手工调参的机制化流程；通用的三维图生成器则未能利用或强制树的结构特征。

Method: 提出 Autoregressive Frontier Expansion 生成框架，通过迭代扩展过程构建树，模拟真实树木的生物生长。每一步使用由 SO(2)-等变图神经网络参数化的流匹配模型，预测当前每个活跃分支是分叉还是终止，从而扩展前沿。

Result: 在皮层神经元与植物树上进行评估，涵盖无条件生成、类别条件生成与形态引导生成。在两个领域上，生成的形态与参考分布高度吻合；在条件实验中，生成结果也与指定目标高度一致。

Conclusion: Autoregressive Frontier Expansion 通过模拟生物生长式的自回归前沿扩展，结合等变图神经网络与流匹配，为树状结构提供了通用且逼真的生成方案，验证了其跨领域（神经元、植物）的有效性与条件可控性。

Abstract: Tree-like branching structures are common in nature, from botanical trees to neurons, blood vessels and respiratory trees. Their branching shape often reflects function, making structural modelling central to understanding how these systems work. Because acquiring real-world 3D data is often expensive or infeasible, realistic generative models are valuable for simulation and data augmentation. Existing morphology-specific models either constrain how topology is generated or rely on hand-tuned, mechanistic procedures. Generic 3D graph generators, by contrast, do not exploit or enforce the structure of trees. We propose Autoregressive Frontier Expansion, a generative framework that constructs trees through an iterative expansion process, simulating the biological growth of real trees. At each step, a flow-matching model parameterised by an SO(2)-equivariant GNN expands the frontier by predicting whether each active branch bifurcates or terminates. We evaluate our method on cortical neurons and botanical trees in unconditional, class-conditioned, and morphology-guided generation. Across both domains, the generated morphologies agree closely with the reference distributions and, in conditional experiments, with the specified targets.

</details>


### [66] [Does Text Steer Neural PDE Surrogates? A Controlled Diagnostic with OperatorCLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38517)
*Aadi Dash,Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出：文本条件神经代理模型误差更低并不能证明其利用了文本含义；通过OperatorCLIP的对照实验（无条件FNO、常量句子FiLM对照、任务描述+对比对齐）在三个物理任务上验证，未发现可靠的语义顺序，强调需要路径对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免仅凭误差降低就错误归因模型具备语义理解能力；厘清文本条件模型的性能提升究竟来自文本含义还是其他混杂因素。

Method: 提出OperatorCLIP框架，对比无条件FNO、常量句子FiLM控制组与固定任务描述（用对比对齐训练）；在Darcy2D、ShallowWater2D、三维可压缩Navier-Stokes（CNS3D）上做三随机种子实验，并进行提示干预与InfoNCE分析。

Result: 常量条件在2D任务上平均测试误差更低；任务文本+对齐相对常量控制在ShallowWater2D与CNS3D均值相近、在Darcy2D更高，且种子间不确定度大；后者同时改变提示内容与损失，无法分离单一效应；从头训练文本编码器且每模型仅见一个描述时，成对InfoNCE无法识别匹配对，最小值为log B；提示干预未显示可靠语义排序。

Conclusion: 该研究是方法学警示，说明需要路径对照；既未证明编码器具备语义能力，也未检验文本在变化物理语境下的有效性。

Abstract: Lower error from a text-conditioned neural surrogate does not, by itself, show that the model uses the meaning of the text. We examine this attribution problem with OperatorCLIP, comparing an unconditioned FNO, a constant-sentence FiLM control, and a fixed task description trained with contrastive alignment. Three-seed experiments cover Darcy2D, ShallowWater2D, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes (CNS3D). Constant conditioning has lower mean test error on both 2D tasks. Relative to this control, task text plus alignment has a similar mean on ShallowWater2D and CNS3D and a higher mean on Darcy2D; these descriptive comparisons have substantial seed uncertainty. The latter comparison changes both prompt content and loss, so it isolates neither effect. The text encoder is trained from scratch, and each conditioned model sees only one description during training. In this regime, pairwise InfoNCE cannot identify matched pairs and has minimum $\log B$. Prompt interventions show no reliable semantic ordering. This methodological caution demonstrates why pathway controls are needed; it neither establishes semantic competence of the encoder nor tests the effectiveness of text under varying physical context.

</details>


### [67] [ShamAN-Q: Shampoo Augmented NanoQuant for Sub-1-bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38521)
*Jonathan Mei,Sang Hyub Kim,Oliver Knitter,Chi Chen,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShamAN-Q是一种亚1比特训练后量化方法，通过用稠密曲率度量替换对角重构几何，提升了NanoQuant的量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NanoQuant使用对角重构几何，可能无法充分捕捉权重间的相关性，限制了极低比特量化下的重构精度。作者希望引入更精确的曲率信息来改善量化质量。

Method: 基于Shampoo优化器范式，对每个线性权重的经验Fisher信息矩阵拟合Kronecker积，通过KL最小化构建Mahalanobis重构损失；连续ADMM更新化为Sylvester方程求解，离散投影和部署格式不变；逐层重新测量输入曲率统计并周期性刷新，同时重新分配各层均匀秩。

Result: 在Qwen3-Base上，约1 bpw时WikiText-2困惑度从27.56降至22.96（0.6B）、19.21降至16.72（1.7B）、14.29降至13.80（4B），并在Eleuther LM Evaluation Harness上匹配或提升零样本准确率；0.6B模型约0.8 bpw即可达到NanoQuant约1.0 bpw的困惑度。

Conclusion: ShamAN-Q在保持部署格式不变的前提下，通过稠密曲率度量显著改善了亚1比特量化的精度，尤其在低比特下表现突出。

Abstract: We introduce ShamAN-Q, a sub-1-bit post-training quantization method that extends NanoQuant by replacing each its diagonal reconstruction geometry with a tractable dense curvature metric, using a general paradigm popularized by the Shampoo optimizer. For each linear weight, ShamAN-Q fits a Kronecker product to the empirical Fisher information matrix of a small calibration set by Kullback--Leibler minimization, forming a Mahalanobis reconstruction loss from the result. The continuous ADMM updates from NanoQuant become solutions to Sylvester equations, while its discrete projection and deployment format remain unchanged. Because the curvature is local to a given set of weights, ShamAN-Q re-measures the input curvature statistic for each layer immediately before layer factorization, periodically refreshing all statistics on the partially quantized model. ShamAN-Q also redistributes the uniform rank from NanoQuant across layers at the same total number of bits. On Qwen3-Base, ShamAN-Q lowers WikiText-2 perplexity at $\approx$1 bpw from 27.56 to 22.96 (0.6B), 19.21 to 16.72 (1.7B), and 14.29 to 13.80 (4B) while matching or improving zero-shot accuracy on the Eleuther LM Evaluation Harness. On 0.6B, ShamAN-Q at $\approx$0.8 bpw matches the published perplexity of NanoQuant at $\approx$1.0 bpw.

</details>


### [68] [MM-FinEval: A Multi-Task Multimodal Benchmark for Real-World Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38523)
*Dong Shu,Yanguang Liu,Huopu Zhang,Saisai Hu,Haiyan Zhao,Hekun Huang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-FinEval，一个面向财报电话会议的多模态金融基准，包含 2045 个标普500会议样本、12 类任务标签、文本/幻灯片/音频三模态输入，并评测了 19 个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准多为单模态或单任务设置，难以评估多模态大模型能否胜任真实金融分析。而财报电话会议预测需要模型同时处理复杂披露、市场预期与微妙沟通信号。

Method: 构建 MM-FinEval 基准，时间跨度为 2019–2022 年；数据含 2045 个标普500财报电话会议作为输入，12 个金融任务标签作为输出；每条输入包含三模态：逐词文本转录、会议演示幻灯片、完整音频。评测 19 个基线模型，分为 Image-Text、Audio-Text、Any-to-Any 三类配置。

Result: 小规模 Any-to-Any 模型在使用全部三模态时表现强劲，甚至优于仅限两模态输入的大型专有模型，说明三模态设计带来了有用且非冗余的信息。

Conclusion: 文本、音频与视觉数据是重要且互补的信号，可模拟专家人类分析师的多模态决策过程，为多模态金融分析提供了严谨的评估框架。

Abstract: Financial forecasting from earnings conference calls requires models to reason over complex corporate disclosures, market expectations, and subtle communication signals. However, existing financial benchmarks are often limited to unimodal inputs or single-task settings, making it difficult to evaluate whether multimodal large language models (LLMs) can support real-world financial analysis. In this paper, we introduce MM-FinEval, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal LLMs across multiple financial tasks. MM-FinEval spans a diverse timeline from 2019 to 2022. The entire proposed dataset contains 2,045 S\&P 500 conference earning calls as inputs and 12 financial task labels as outputs. Each input contains three modalities: a word-to-word text transcript of the earning call, the corresponding presentation slides used during the call, and the entire audio recording. To establish a rigorous evaluation framework, we analyze 19 baseline models across three distinct model categories: Image-Text, Audio-Text, and Any-to-Any configurations. We observe that small-size Any-to-Any models processing all three modalities achieve strong performance, even when compared against larger proprietary models restricted to two-modality inputs. This indicates that our tri-modal dataset design introduces useful, non-redundant information. These results validate that text, audio, and visual data serve as important, complementary signals that mimic the decision-making process of expert human analysts.

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting scaling laws for reward optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38526)
*Ali Aouad,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于AI对齐中奖励模型优化的联合缩放定律，指出性能大致按Θ(√(min{log(M),K}))缩放，其中M是训练数据的比较数量，K是策略的散度预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未清晰识别性能如何同时随训练数据量和散度预算缩放。优化对代理奖励模型时，超过一定预算会出现过度优化（奖励黑客），因为优化的是代理奖励而非真实奖励。代理奖励的准确性取决于训练用的偏好数据量，因此需要联合考虑数据量与散度预算对性能的影响。

Method: 作者建立了一个信息论模型来推导上界，并证明该上界可通过构造性过程紧致达成。随后使用真实标注设置进行广泛实证评估：由大型700亿参数金标准奖励模型生成反馈数据，从较弱模型（0.6B到4B）训练代理奖励模型。

Result: 缩放定律拟合效果优秀（R²从97%到99%），优于其他替代规范，并在模型规模、噪声和优化过程（best-of-N或策略倾斜）中保持稳健。证据表明奖励优化类似于一个简单的选择任务：使用噪声偏好反馈从IID高斯随机变量序列中进行选择。

Conclusion: 性能大致按Θ(√(min{log(M),K}))缩放，该定律在理论上成立且实验上高度准确，表明奖励优化可类比为使用噪声偏好反馈的简单选择任务。

Abstract: Scaling laws for optimization against reward models in AI alignment have pinned down how performance depends on optimization effort---measured by a KL-divergence budget relative to a reference policy. Beyond a certain budget, over-optimization (or reward hacking) can arise: because we optimize against a proxy reward model (distinct from true rewards), performance can plateau or degrade. Naturally, the proxy reward's accuracy depends on how much preference data (often in the form of pairwise comparisons) was used to train it. However, existing research does not cleanly identify how performance jointly scales with the amount of training data and the divergence budget. Our main contribution is to provide an empirically accurate and theoretically grounded scaling law in such context. Performance roughly scales as $Θ(\sqrt{\min\{\log(M),K\}})$, where $M$ is the number of comparisons in training data and $K$ is the policy's divergence budget. We develop an information-theoretic model to establish this upper bound and prove it is tightly achievable through a constructive procedure. Informed by this, we conduct extensive empirical evaluations using a real-world annotation setup, whereby a large 70B gold reward model generates feedback data and proxy reward models are trained from less capable models (0.6B to 4B). Our scaling law provides an excellent fit (R2 from 97\% to 99\%), outperforms alternative specifications, and remains robust across model sizes, noise, and optimization procedures (best-of-$N$ or policy tilting). Our evidence suggests that reward optimization is analogous to a surprisingly simple selection task: choosing from a sequence of IID Gaussian random variables using noisy preference feedback.

</details>


### [70] [Does This Action Still Explain the Task? Reverse Scoring for Diffusion Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38536)
*Jiacheng Qiu,Christopher E. Mower,Jan Peters,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Matthieu Zimmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示扩散大语言模型(dLLM)在具身智能体任务中反复陷入"重试循环"的机制，并提出一种免训练补救方法 Reflect Reverse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 通过并行解码有望打破自回归智能体的串行延迟瓶颈，但评估显示其效率优势无法转化为具身智能体能力：智能体会不断重复已失败的动作。作者希望从机制上解释这一失败，并给出无需训练的解决方案。

Method: 作者将重试循环归因于掩码解码的自适应性：采样器提交高置信位置、推迟不确定位置，而在失败状态下上下文已为被推迟的决策提供了高置信填充（即失败动作本身），导致重试被提交而失败反馈从未被面对。作者将该动作分布扭曲建模为"任务无关的腐蚀"：语境显著的动作获得膨胀概率，膨胀因子依赖状态与动作但不依赖任务。基于此模型分析一个不变性命题：任务无关因子在反向条件（给定状态与候选动作时任务的可能性，即任务后验）中精确抵消。掩码 dLLM 可通过掩蔽任务 token 并去噪，原生地评估该反向条件，每个候选只需几次并行前向。据此实例化为规则 Reflect Reverse。

Result: 在四个多轮具身基准上评估，Reflect Reverse 相比前向打分基线提升了任务成功率和进展率。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的具身失败源于掩码解码的自适应提交机制；通过评估任务后验可抵消任务无关的腐蚀因子，Reflect Reverse 作为免训练方法有效缓解重试循环，提升具身智能体表现。

Abstract: Diffusion-based large language models (dLLMs) promise to break the sequential latency bottleneck of autoregressive agents through parallel decoding, but recent evaluations show this efficiency does not transfer to embodied agentic competence: dLLM-backed agents repeatedly fall into retry loops, re-issuing an action long after it has failed. We give a mechanistic account of this failure and a training-free remedy. We trace the retry loop to the adaptivity of masked decoding: the sampler commits the positions it is most confident about and defers the uncertain ones, and at a failure state the context already offers a confident fill for the deferred decision, i.e. the failed action itself, so the retry is committed without the failure feedback ever being confronted. We model the resulting distortion of the action distribution as a task-blind corruption: contextually salient actions (e.g., the action just taken) receive inflated probability by a factor that depends on the state and the action but not on the task. Under this model, we analyse an invariance proposition: the task-blind factor cancels exactly from the reverse conditional, i.e. the likelihood of the task given the state and a candidate action, which coincides with the task posterior of an idealized uncorrupted model. Masked dLLMs evaluate the reverse conditional natively, unlike autoregressive models, by masking the task tokens and denoising, at the cost of a few parallel passes per candidate. We instantiate the rule as Reflect Reverse and evaluate it on four multi-turn embodied benchmarks, where it improves task success and progression rates over forward-scoring baselines.

</details>


### [71] [ReLaG: A Scalable Framework Generalizing Random Splits to Data with Latent Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38538)
*Anthony Lavertu,Jacob Cote,Sophie Gobeil,Jacques Corbeil,Isabeau Premont-Schwarz,Pascal Germain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLaG，一个模态无关的框架，通过层次潜变量过程建模样本相关性，并利用邻近图与社区检测推断相关样本组，从而生成独立的训练-测试子集，避免随机划分导致的乐观泛化估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机划分在样本间存在相关性时（如生化研究）会产生非独立的训练-测试子集，导致泛化估计过于乐观。现有关系感知方法在数据集规模上受限，难以应用于实际大规模数据。

Method: ReLaG通过层次潜变量过程建模样本相关性，使用邻近图与社区检测推断相关样本组，实现独立划分；并提出无标签程序，自适应调整划分分辨率以匹配生产数据与部署场景。

Result: 在分子和蛋白质数据集上，ReLaG与现有关系感知方法表现相当，但扩展性显著更好，可在以往不切实际的数据集规模上完成划分；其推断的组还能廉价估计有效数据集大小，支持多样性感知的数据集扩展。

Conclusion: ReLaG是一个开源、模态无关的框架，可通过pip安装，能有效生成独立训练-测试子集并提供有效数据集规模估计，助力更可靠和可扩展的评估。

Abstract: Random splitting can yield non-independent train--test subsets when a dataset contains related samples, as is common in certain applications such as biochemical studies. This leads to overly optimistic generalization estimates. Here, we introduce ReLaG, a modality-agnostic framework that models sample relatedness through a hierarchical latent-variable process and infers groups of related samples using proximity graphs and community detection to produce independent train--test subsets. Across molecular and protein datasets, ReLaG matches existing relation-aware methods while scaling substantially better, enabling splits at previously impractical dataset sizes. We further introduce a label-free procedure that adapts the splitting resolution to production data, aligning evaluation with the intended deployment setting. ReLaG's inferred groups provide a cheap estimate of effective dataset size, enabling diversity-aware dataset scaling. ReLaG is open source and can be installed with pip install relag.

</details>


### [72] [When a Flatness Proxy Is Not a Function: Robustness Certificates and Training Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38540)
*Vicente Opazo,Jose Calatayud-Mateu,Cristobal Rojas,Cristian Buc Calderon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了"曲率上界有效性"与"鲁棒证书/内在训练干预正当性"，证明相对平坦度代理在这两种用途中并不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最后一层相对平坦度代理（proxy）常被同时用作鲁棒性证书和内在训练干预依据，但作者认为一个有效的曲率上界本身并不足以支撑这两种用途，需要重新检验其正当性。

Method: 理论分析两方面：(1) 在有限全局经验风险最小点处考察保留的证书表达式，并用全局有效、规范不变的特征空间修复；(2) 分析常见行softmax平移对预测、收缩性和代理值的影响，提出行中心化以得到轨道最小化上界。实验上做了45组配对单步测试（算法类与图像模型）以及长时程CIFAR-10实验。

Result: 经验风险稳定性不能消除逐点一阶损失项：在有限全局经验风险最小点处，保留的证书表达式低估损失增幅超过210倍。常见行softmax平移保持预测与精确收缩性，却使代理无界；标准参考类选择平均使代理翻倍。在至少三类的固定特征样例中，标量重调通常无法对齐诱导的概率更新。行中心化给出轨道最小化上界，并在此对称性下恢复数值与全模型梯度不变性。45组配对测试中，放大的平移使原始正则化预测器分离，而商正则化预测器保持对齐。CIFAR-10长时程实验显示泛化被显著且可逆地抑制，而"记忆后选择性延迟"的证据不一致。

Conclusion: 作为曲率上界的有效性，本身并不能正当化将其反演为鲁棒性证书，也不能正当化将其微分作为内在训练干预。

Abstract: A valid curvature upper bound need not justify either a robustness certificate or an intervention on an intrinsic predictor property. We demonstrate this distinction for a last-layer relative-flatness proxy used in both settings. First, empirical-risk stationarity does not eliminate pointwise first-order loss terms: at a finite global empirical-risk minimum, the retained certificate expression underestimates a loss increase by over $210\times$. We derive a globally valid, gauge-invariant feature-space repair. Second, common-row softmax shifts preserve predictions and the exact contraction while making the proxy unbounded. Even standard reference-class choices double it on average relative to the centered representation. For a single fixed-feature example with at least three classes, scalar retuning generically cannot align the induced probability updates. Row centering gives the orbit-minimized bound and restores value and full-model gradient invariance under this symmetry. Across 45 paired one-step tests on algorithmic and image models, amplified shifts separate raw-regularized predictors while quotient-regularized predictors remain aligned. Long-horizon CIFAR-10 experiments show substantial, reversible suppression of generalization, while evidence for selective delay after memorization is less consistent. Together, these results show that validity as a curvature upper bound does not by itself justify either inversion into a robustness certificate or differentiation into an intrinsic training intervention.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Universal Wasserstein Barycenters through Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38547)
*Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Universal Barycenter Approximation 问题并提出 BaryFM，一个流匹配模型，可传输边缘测度到 Wasserstein simplex 中任意重心，训练后可经 ODE 采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法多针对固定权重向量计算 Wasserstein 重心，对 simplex 上整个重心族的近似研究不足；需要一种能一次训练覆盖整个 Wasserstein simplex 的方法。

Method: 提出 BaryFM，基于 flow matching，将边缘测度传输到 Wasserstein simplex 中任意 barycenter；训练完成后通过求解 ODE 从 simplex 内任意重心采样。

Result: 在 4 个下游任务（domain adaptation、generalization、Bayesian posterior aggregation、algorithmic fairness）上验证；在 10 个 domain adaptation 基准上，BaryFM 在 15 种对比方法中取得最佳平均排名，并匹配或超越非通用求解器。

Conclusion: BaryFM 为整个 Wasserstein simplex 上的通用重心近似提供了可行方案，并在多项任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Defining a weighted mean over probability measures under probability metrics is a central tool in probabilistic machine learning. Under the Wasserstein metric, these are called \emph{Wasserstein barycenters}. While most approaches compute barycenters for a fixed weight vector, approximating the whole family of barycenters over the simplex, which we call the \emph{Wasserstein simplex}, remains underexplored. We refer to this problem as \emph{Universal Barycenter Approximation}, and propose \texttt{BaryFM}, a flow matching model transporting the marginal measures into any barycenter in the Wasserstein simplex. Once trained, the network can draw samples from measures in the Wasserstein simplex through an ordinary differential equation. We validate our method on 4 downstream tasks: domain adaptation, generalization, Bayesian posterior aggregation and algorithmic fairness. \texttt{BaryFM} achieves the best average rank among 15 competing methods across 10 domain adaptation benchmarks, matching or surpassing non-universal solvers.

</details>


### [74] [The Advantages of Fresh Sketching for Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38565)
*Linkai Ma,Qilin Li,Petros Drineas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究迭代随机求解器中"重用同一草图"与"每步重新采样"哪种更有优势，证明在欠约束迭代岭回归的列采样中，新鲜草图具有可证明的优势，并提出残差感知采样规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去25年，草图与采样被广泛用于加速大规模回归。在迭代随机求解器中，核心设计选择是重用同一草图还是每步抽取新鲜随机性。对于（欠约束）迭代岭回归的列采样，新鲜草图是否具有可证明优势一直是未解问题。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 新鲜草图允许仅沿当前残差解分析误差，而非在整个Gram矩阵上一致分析。这一"方向性"视角对杠杆分数采样和岭杠杆分数采样给出更紧的收敛保证，并导出残差感知采样规则。通过最小化相关草图矩阵-向量乘积的方差，作者推导出oracle分布及其实际近似，包括带（稍弱）收敛保证的混合采样分布。

Result: 理论分析表明新鲜草图可带来更快的收敛保证。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验，包括在Qwen2.5表示上的岭探测，均支持该理论，显示收敛速度显著加快。

Conclusion: 新鲜草图在迭代岭回归的列采样中具有可证明优势；方向性误差分析与残差感知采样规则能显著加速收敛，实验验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: Over the past 25 years, sketching and sampling have become widely used tools for accelerating large-scale regression. In iterative randomized solvers, a basic design choice is whether to $\textit{reuse}$ the same sketch or draw $\textit{fresh}$ randomness at every step. For (under-constrained) iterative ridge regression with column sampling, whether fresh sketches offer provable advantages has remained open: $\textit{We show that they do.}$ Fresh sketching lets us analyze error only along the current residual solution, rather than uniformly over the entire Gram matrix. This directional view yields sharper convergence guarantees for leverage score and ridge leverage score sampling and, more importantly, leads to residual-aware sampling rules. By minimizing the variance of the relevant sketched matrix-vector product, we derive an oracle distribution and practical approximations to the oracle distribution, including a mixture sampling distribution with (somewhat weaker) convergence guarantees. Experiments on synthetic and real data, including ridge probes on Qwen2.5 representations, support our theory, showing substantially faster convergence.

</details>

### [75] [Optimize, Learn, Refine: Whole-Body Grasping and Pick-and-Throw with a Spiral Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38202)
*Marwah Basuhai,Tingcong Liu,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过基于结果的动作空间优化，使软体连续体机器人从初始未抓取状态实现全身抓取与抓取-投掷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续体机器人可利用分布式柔顺实现全身操作，但通过变换接触来合成行为仍然困难，尤其是从初始未抓取状态完成抓取和投掷。

Method: 提出基于结果的动作空间优化：抓取用末端角扫掠与身体-物体包覆度量化，投掷进一步加入释放方向对齐与最小释放速度；由于动作到结果映射非光滑，采用无导数CMA-ES并结合“优化-学习-精修”框架，任务条件预测器学习热启动，CMA-ES再精修。

Result: 仿真中实现492/500成功抓取（98.4%），三个方向投掷成功率分别为98%、97%、94%；学习初始化将抓取成功率从78.6%提升至98.4%，并将CMA-ES中位 rollout 次数从1184降至816。硬件实验50次抓取成功率100%，30次抓取-投掷成功率100%，每个方向重复10次。

Conclusion: 仿真与硬件结果共同表明，该框架在模拟和物理全身操作任务中均有效。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots can exploit distributed compliance for whole-body manipulation, but synthesizing behavior through changing contacts remains difficult. We address whole-body grasping and pick-and-throw from an initially ungrasped state through outcome-based actuation-space optimization. Grasping is quantified by tip angular sweep and body-object enclosure, while throwing further incorporates release-direction alignment and minimum release speed. These objectives allow grasping, acceleration, and release to emerge from compliant interaction without prescribing contact forces, contact locations, or body configurations. Because the resulting actuation-to-outcome mapping is nonsmooth, we utilize derivative-free CMA-ES within an optimize-learn-refine framework. CMA-ES generates solutions for sampled conditions, a task-conditioned predictor learns warm starts, and CMA-ES refines them for unseen conditions. In simulation, the method achieves 492/500 successful grasps (98.4%) and success rates of 98%, 97%, and 94% across three directional throwing trials. Learned initialization increases grasping success from 78.6% to 98.4% while reducing the median rollout count from 1184 to 816 in CMA-ES. Hardware experiments achieve a 100% grasping success rate across 50 executions and a 100% pick-and-throw success rate across 30 executions, with 10 repetitions per direction. Together, these simulation and hardware results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both simulated and physical whole-body manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [76] [Fiatlux: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Humanoid Ladder Climbing and Light-Bulb Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38216)
*Pavel Bushuyeu,Yujin Chen,Anton Nikolaev,Brian Shu,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Fiatlux——首个在单一长时程任务中同时评测人形机器人垂直移动与在脆弱负载上精细操作的灯泡更换基准，基于 NVIDIA Isaac Lab，含12个子任务、难度加权评分与参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅分别评测桌面操作、平层家庭活动或人形行走与操作等独立任务组，尚无基准能在同一长时程回合中同时考察垂直移动与对脆弱物品的精细操作，因此缺少综合评估人形机器人长时程作业能力的标准化平台。

Method: 构建 Fiatlux 基准，基于 NVIDIA Isaac Lab。单个回合中 Unitree G1 人形机器人需将梯子置于天花板或墙面灯具下方、攀爬、将插座中的旧灯泡更换为新灯泡，并把旧灯泡放入回收箱。回合被分解为12个子任务环境，采用难度加权门控打分，支持部分得分。观测分为标准模式（机器人可感知或估计的信号）与特权模式（精确仿真状态）。提供评测协议及参考基线实现，涵盖 RSL-RL PPO、零样本 NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 VLA 模型与全身控制器，并提供用于定义与校验成功门控的遥操作录制数据。

Result: 成功判定要求新灯泡安装到位、旧灯泡被妥善处理、两者均未掉落且未超过脆弱性约束；未完全达成者可获部分得分。基准代码与遥操作录制数据已通过 fiatlux-bench.github.io 公开。

Conclusion: Fiatlux 填补了长时程人形机器人垂直移动与脆弱物体精细操作综合评测的空白，提供可复现的评测协议、多样化参考基线与公开数据集，为后续研究建立统一比较基础。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks evaluate tabletop manipulation, flat-floor household activity, or humanoid locomotion and manipulation as separate task groups; none scores vertical mobility and dexterous work on a fragile payload in one long-horizon episode. We present Fiatlux, a light-bulb replacement benchmark built on NVIDIA Isaac Lab. In one episode, a Unitree G1 humanoid positions a step ladder under a ceiling or wall fixture, climbs it, exchanges a spent bulb in a socket for a fresh one, and leaves the spent one in a disposal crate. We decompose the episode into twelve subtask environments scored on difficulty-weighted gates. The goal is a successful replacement, with the fresh bulb seated, the spent one disposed of, neither dropped, and a fragility bound not crossed. Runs that fall short can earn partial credit. Observations are split into a standard mode (signals a physical robot could sense or estimate) and a privileged mode (exact simulator state). We specify the evaluation protocol and provide reference baseline implementations spanning RSL-RL PPO, zero-shot NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and whole-body controllers. Additionally, we provide the teleoperated recordings used to specify and check the success gates. The benchmark code and the teleoperated recordings are available at fiatlux-bench.github.io.

</details>


### [77] [SynIL: Leveraging Synergy for Offline Imitation Learning from Imperfect Demonstration Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38225)
*Yuto Tanaka,Kyo Kutsuzawa,Martina Doku,Dai Owaki,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynIL 框架，利用动作协同（motor synergy）的量化来生成稠密、逐转移级的奖励信号，实现自动化、无需标签的示教质量评估，从而提升受劣质数据污染的模仿学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习虽能从大规模示教数据中直接获取复杂技能，但数据集含次优或噪声示教时性能严重下降。已有质量评估方法多依赖人工预选专家参考数据或任务特定启发式，难以扩展。作者受神经科学证据启发——动作协同（运动中的低维协调结构）与运动熟练度直接相关——提出无需人工干预的质量评估方案。

Method: 提出 SynIL（Synergy-based Imitation Learning），在离线强化学习场景下进行自动化、无标签的示教质量评估。算法通过量化动作协同的表现程度，并借助自监督奖励回归生成稠密、transition 级别的奖励信号。

Result: 在 D4RL 运动基准与多人类 Robomimic 操作数据集上，协同导出的奖励与真实奖励高度相关；SynIL 显著优于行为克隆（BC），并与使用真实环境奖励训练的离线强化学习性能相当，在稀疏奖励的人类遥操作场景中甚至更优。

Conclusion: SynIL 证明了以动作协同为核心、无需标签的示教质量评估可行且有效，可作为通用奖励信号来源提升离线模仿学习在含噪数据下的鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex skills directly from massive demonstration datasets, but its performance degrades severely when datasets are contaminated with suboptimal or noisy demonstrations. While prior quality-assessment methods attempt to filter or reweight data, they typically rely on manual pre-selection of expert reference data or task-specific heuristics, limiting scalability. To address this challenge, we introduce SynIL (Synergy-based Imitation Learning), a novel framework for automated, label-free demonstration quality assessment in offline reinforcement learning. Grounded in neuroscientific evidence that motor synergy, a low-dimensional coordinated structure in movement, correlates directly with motor proficiency, SynIL algorithmically quantifies synergy manifestation to generate dense, transition-level reward signals via self-supervised reward regression. Comprehensive evaluations on D4RL locomotion benchmarks and multi-human Robomimic manipulation datasets demonstrate that synergy-derived rewards correlate strongly with ground-truth rewards. Furthermore, SynIL substantially outperforms Behavior Cloning (BC) and achieves performance comparable to, and in sparse-reward human teleoperation scenarios, superior to, offline reinforcement learning trained on true environment rewards.

</details>


### [78] [A Two-Echelon Covering Tour Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones for Post-Disaster Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38227)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Rajesh Piplani,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两梯队覆盖巡游车辆路径问题（2E-CTVRP）用于灾后救援物资配送，建立混合整数线性规划模型并设计混合元启发式算法 GRASP-ILS-PR 求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后救援物资配送需要兼顾卡车与无人机的协同运输，且需快速、公平地覆盖受灾点。现有研究较少同时考虑卫星分配、卡车路径排序与集群分配的联合优化，因此需要建立新问题模型并开发高效求解方法。

Method: 将 2E-CTVRP 建模为混合整数线性规划：第一梯队卡车将物资与无人机从中心仓库运至灾区外围卫星点；第二梯队无人机从卫星点并行飞往由受灾点聚类得到的受害集群质心配送，卡车在卫星点等待无人机返回。提出混合元启发式 GRASP-ILS-PR，融合贪婪随机构造、单轨迹搜索以及针对集群-卫星分配的周期性路径重连。

Result: 在包含 110 个实例的新基准集上，GRASP-ILS-PR 在 30 秒内找到所有已知最优解，在多数较大实例上匹配或超越商业求解器一小时所得解，在 50 个卫星点的实例上改进幅度最高达 11%。算法明显优于传统 GRASP 结合演化路径重连的方法，并比使用相同搜索组件的模拟退火算法结果更稳定。车队配置分析表明，少数大型卡车在多数实例中最优累计到达时间，而多辆小型卡车在每个实例中都能更早、更公平地送达物资。

Conclusion: GRASP-ILS-PR 能高效求解 2E-CTVRP，并在解质量与稳定性上优于对比算法；车队规模与车辆大小需在累计到达时间与配送及时性、公平性之间权衡。

Abstract: We introduce the two-echelon covering tour vehicle routing problem (2E-CTVRP) for the distribution of relief supplies after a disaster. In the first echelon, a fleet of trucks transports supplies and drones from a central depot to satellites at the periphery of the affected area. In the second echelon, drones launched in parallel from the satellites deliver the supplies to the centroids of victim clusters, which are obtained by clustering the victim locations, and each truck waits at a satellite until its drones have returned. The problem combines the assignment of satellites to trucks, the sequencing of the truck routes, and the assignment of clusters to satellites, and minimizes the sum of the arrival times of the trucks at the satellites and at the depot. We formulate the 2E-CTVRP as a mixed integer linear program and propose a hybrid metaheuristic, GRASP-ILS-PR, which combines greedy randomized construction, a single-trajectory search, and periodic path relinking on the assignment of clusters to satellites. On a new benchmark set of 110 instances, GRASP-ILS-PR finds all known optimal solutions within 30 seconds, matches or improves on the one-hour solutions of a commercial solver on most larger instances, and improves on them by up to 11% for instances with 50 satellites. It clearly outperforms a conventional GRASP with evolutionary path relinking and yields more consistent results than a simulated annealing algorithm that uses the same search components. An analysis of two fleet configurations shows that few large trucks minimize the cumulative arrival time in most instances, whereas many small trucks deliver supplies to the victims earlier and more equitably in every instance.

</details>


### [79] [TALK-Dem: Benchmarking Embodied Task Planning under Dementia-Associated Communication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38371)
*Guangxin Zhao,Yiran Hu,Yuan Cao,Chenxi Jiang,Jianfei Yang,Yegang Du,Yasuyuki Taki,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Lin Gu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出TALK-Dem基准，评估LLM驱动机器人任务规划在痴呆相关言语交流下的鲁棒性，并提出CARE方法缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器人任务规划器假设用户指令清晰、完整且聚焦任务，但在真实场景中，尤其是认知障碍用户（如痴呆患者PLWD）交流时，规划器易出错甚至带来人身安全风险，缺乏相应评估基准。

Method: 构建TALK-Dem基准，包含4800条指令，覆盖五种典型交流模式（指代不精确、对象替换、空语、话题漂移、侵入），分三个强度等级；在六种开源LLM上进行实验；并提出Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE)方法，通过检索以往已解决的相关任务来提供任务特定的解释与规划上下文。

Result: 六种开源LLM相比理想指令性能最多下降22.3个百分点；CARE在六种模型上普遍优于标准提示基线，相比vanilla prompt平均任务成功率提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 强调了评估交流鲁棒性及开发有效适应策略对本地可部署辅助机器人的重要性。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven robot task planners rely on a taken-for-granted assumption of an ideal user whose instructions are clear, complete, and task-focused. However, when interacting with real-world users, especially those experiencing cognitive impairments, such as people living with dementia (PLWD), the planners often make mistakes and even pose physical safety risks. We proposed TALK-Dem (Talking Attributes and Linguistic Knowledge in Dementia), the first benchmark for evaluating LLM-driven robot task planning under dementia-associated verbal communication. TALK-Dem contains 4,800 instructions and covers five typical communication patterns, including Referential Imprecision, Object Substitution, Empty Speech, Topic Drift, and Intrusion, at three intensity levels. Experiments across six open-weight LLMs reveal a substantial robustness gap. Across communication patterns, open-weight models exhibited performance drops of up to 22.3 percentage points compared to ideal instructions. This revealed a critical gap and even danger for real-world applications, especially in assistive robotics, where locally deployable models are necessary due to privacy concerns and connectivity constraints. To mitigate this issue, we proposed the Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE) method, which retrieves relevant previously resolved tasks to provide task-specific interpretation and planning context. CARE generally outperformed standard prompting baselines across the six open-weight models, improving average task success by 18.1 percentage points over the vanilla prompt. These results highlighted the importance of both evaluating communication robustness and developing effective adaptation strategies for locally deployable assistive robots. The TALK-Dem dataset is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TALK-Dem-A6B3/.

</details>


### [80] [GestAdapt: Workspace-Conditioned Co-Speech Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38400)
*Bosong Ding,Xianglin Zhang,Miao Xin,Murat Kirtay,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GestAdapt框架，将协同言语手势生成以指定腕部工作空间为条件，使机器人在受限空间中生成自然手势，而非事后修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人协同言语手势不仅需适配语音与本体形态，还需适配可用工作空间。同一语音可对应不同手势，机器人应针对空间约束（如墙边）直接生成合适动作，而非修正无约束手势。

Method: GestAdapt通过共享运动表示学习六个互补的协同言语语料库，并将生成条件设定为规定的腕部工作空间，同时支持向不同机器人本体重定向。

Result: 定量评估显示生成动作贴近真实运动分布并满足工作空间约束。用户研究中，受限空间生成手势质量得分3.24/5，高于无工作空间变体（2.43/5），低于参考动作（3.68/5）。真实机器人（Reachy2）评估中，本框架生成动作在69.7%对比中排名第一，优于基线及事后约束的重定向真实动作。

Conclusion: 结果支持在生成阶段就适配可用工作空间，而非事后修改无约束轨迹以满足约束，以免损害手势自然性。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures for robots must adapt not only to speech and embodiment, but also to the workspace available for performing the motion. Since the same speech can be accompanied by different gestures, a robot can respond to workspace constraints, e.g., gestures for speech next to a wall. In these scenarios, the robot should gesture in a suitable motion rather than simply correcting an unconstrained one. To achieve this goal, we present GestAdapt, a workspace-conditioned framework that conditions co-speech gesture generation on a prescribed wrist workspace. The GestAdapt framework learns from six complementary co-speech corpora through a shared motion representation and supports retargeting to different robot embodiments. Quantitative evaluation shows that generated motions remain close to the real-motion distribution while respecting the workspace. In a user study, gestures generated under modified workspace constraints receive a mean quality score of 3.24/5, above our no-workspace variant (2.43/5) and below the reference motions (3.68/5). In a real robot evaluation, all compared motions are retargeted to the Reachy2 humanoid robot under identical workspace constraints. Motions generated with our framework rank first in 69.7\% of comparisons, higher than our no-workspace variant baseline and retargeted ground-truth motions constrained afterward. Overall, the results support adapting gestures to the available workspace during generation, rather than modifying unconstrained trajectories afterward to satisfy workspace constraints, potentially compromising gesture naturalness.

</details>


### [81] [Memorize, Adapt, Ignore: Diagnosing Robot Learning Mechanisms under Training Data Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38401)
*Ke Zhang,Danica J. Sutherland,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究，利用经验神经正切核（NTK）揭示训练数据变异对机器人操作策略鲁棒性的影响机制，并给出设计DR方案的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据变异（如域随机化DR或模仿学习示教）是提升机器人操作策略鲁棒性的主要手段，但其内在机制尚不清楚，实践者通常只能通过昂贵的试错来选择随机化参数。

Method: 在多个设定下（ManiSkill的pick-and-place强化学习、LIBERO与RoboTwin上VLA模型微调）对物体尺寸、颜色、类型及场景光照和语言提示进行随机化，考察模型行为与内部表征，并以经验NTK作为主要诊断工具。

Result: NTK可区分内部学习机制的转变：变异不足时策略“记忆”不同情形（如分别学大立方体和小立方体的做法），变异充分时则“适应”当前情形；基于NTK的信噪比还能判别策略是否学会“忽略”任务无关因素（如将蓝红立方体同等对待，而非学成两个子策略）。

Conclusion: 这些诊断可用于指导DR方案设计、模型选择与捷径学习检测；作者进一步比较了不同表征，并用基于ACT的模仿学习在真实硬件实验中验证了结论。

Abstract: Training data variation, whether through designing a domain randomization (DR) scheme in simulation or curating demonstrations for imitation learning, is a primary lever for improving the robustness of robotic manipulation policies. Yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and practitioners typically select randomization parameters through expensive trial and error. We investigate these mechanisms through a series of case studies, randomizing object size, color, and type as well as scene lighting and linguistic prompts across settings including pick-and-place RL in ManiSkill and fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) models on LIBERO and RoboTwin. We examine both model behavior and internal representations, using the empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) as our primary diagnostic tool. We show that the NTK distinguishes a shift in the internal learning mechanism from \textit{memorizing} different situations with insufficient variation (e.g.\ learning what to do for a large cube, and what to do for a small cube) to \textit{adapting} to the situation at hand with sufficient variation. An NTK-based signal-to-noise ratio also helps distinguish when policies have learned to \emph{ignore} task-irrelevant factors (e.g.\ treating blue and red cubes identically, instead of learning a blue sub-policy and a red sub-policy). We use these diagnostics to develop practical guidance for designing DR schemes, selecting models, and detecting shortcut learning. We further compare different kinds of representations and validate our findings with real-world hardware experiments using ACT-based imitation learning.

</details>


### [82] [Draft: A Parametric Tool for Robot Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38405)
*David Nguyen,Marcelo Coelho,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Draft是一种参数化生成工具，可将串行链的参数化树自动编译为仿真就绪的MJCF模型，无需CAD，并用真实机器人数据锚定设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人性能受限于形态与控制的联合迭代成本：每次CAD改动都需先转为仿真模型才能开始控制工作。现有协同设计方法多局限于单一平台，且缺乏真实数据支撑设计合理性。

Method: 提出Draft，其通用引擎可将任意串行链参数化树编译为仿真就绪的MJCF模型；通过拟合114个执行器和49份已发布机器人描述的调查趋势，为设计自由参数提供真实硬件锚定；并用四款现成机器人的孪生模型整体验证这些趋势。

Result: 四款现成机器人孪生模型的质量与真实值相比，几何平均折叠误差为1.10倍；并通过两阶段强化学习课程评估三种四足机器人，展示了Draft揭示设计权衡的能力。

Conclusion: Draft实现了无需CAD的仿真就绪模型快速生成，并借助真实数据锚定设计参数，为形态-控制协同设计与权衡探索提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Robot performance is often limited by the cost of iterating on morphology and control together, since every computer-aided design (CAD) change has to be carried into a simulation-ready model before control work begins. Co-design methods attempt to close this gap, but each uses a model generator written for a single platform or lack the use of real-world data to suggest that designs are plausible. We present Draft, a parametric generation tool whose generalized engine compiles any parametric tree of serial chains into a simulation-ready MJCF model, without CAD. It allows engineers to explore design tradeoffs through easily adjustable models and evaluate how changes influence controller performance. Draft grounds the free parameters of each design using trends fitted to a survey of $114$ actuators and $49$ published robot descriptions, so that a generated robot is anchored to real-world hardware. We validate those trends wholistically by building twins of four off-the-shelf robots, whose masses agree to $1.10\times$ geometric mean fold error. Finally, we demonstrate how Draft exposes design tradeoffs by evaluating three quadrupeds through a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum.

</details>


### [83] [PneuTac: Tactile Manipulation with Soft Pneumatic Robots via Unified MPM-Gaussian Splatting Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38418)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Joe Watson,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PneuTac是一个统一框架，用物质点法（MPM）与3D高斯溅射（3DGS）联合建模软体气动机器人和触觉传感器，通过视觉化实到仿建模与代理模型训练动作/感知网络，在真实接触密集任务上优于仅用真实数据的基线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动机器人可通过柔顺性实现安全接触，视触觉传感器能提供高分辨率触觉感知，二者在精细操作中潜力巨大。但带柔顺机器人的触觉操作学习受限于缺乏高效仿真：现有仿真器通常将软体机器人与触觉传感器分开建模，且存在难以高效克服的巨大标定差距。

Method: 提出PneuTac统一框架：用物质点法（MPM）建模软体机器人与可变形触觉膜的动力学，用3D高斯溅射（3DGS）进行渲染；采用简单的基于视觉的方法完成实到仿建模；随后训练动作与感知网络，借助代理模型实现高效仿真；并以此驱动一条触觉引导的流水线在仿真中采集演示数据。

Result: 在定制的带触觉传感指尖的气动软指上进行实验，并开展额外的跨设备评估，结果表明PneuTac能够准确建模带触觉传感器的软体机器人；在三个真实世界接触密集的柔顺操作任务上，用仿真增强演示训练的策略优于在相同真实数据上训练的基线。

Conclusion: PneuTac可作为柔顺硬件上触觉操作任务的实用框架，验证了统一仿真与触觉传感建模对软体气动机器人操作学习的有效性。

Abstract: Soft robots and tactile sensors have demonstrated great potential in delicate manipulation tasks. Soft pneumatic robots enable safe contact through compliance, and vision-based tactile sensors offer high-resolution touch perception. However, learning tactile manipulation with compliant robots has been challenging, bottlenecked by the lack of efficient simulation. Existing simulators typically model them in isolation, and exhibit large calibration gaps that are difficult to overcome efficiently. We present PneuTac, a unified framework for tactile-feedback manipulation with soft pneumatic robots. We leverage the material point method (MPM) for modelling the dynamics of the soft robot and the deformable tactile membrane, and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) for rendering. Real-to-sim modelling is done with a simple vision-based method, to then train action and perception networks for efficient simulation with surrogate models. We use the framework to drive a tactile-guided pipeline to collect demonstrations in simulation. Through experiments on a custom-designed pneumatic soft finger with a tactile sensing tip, together with additional cross-device evaluations, we show that PneuTac is capable of accurately modelling soft robots with tactile sensors, and that policies trained with simulation-augmented demonstrations outperform baselines trained on the same real data on three real-world contact-rich compliant manipulation tasks, making it a practical framework for tactile manipulation on compliant hardware.

</details>


### [84] [A Reachability-based Safety Certificate for Dynamical System Motion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38421)
*Aditya Vats,Tianyi Xia,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于后向可达管的值函数证书，用于为动态系统（DS）策略的安全性提供保证，避免局部调制或几何控制障碍函数方法导致的碰撞与伪吸引子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统策略在未知环境中部署时需要局部重塑以保证安全，但现有调制或几何CBF等局部策略在复杂障碍几何和DS下可能导致不可避免的碰撞或伪吸引子，因此需要更可靠的安全认证方法。

Method: 从后向可达管概念出发构造值函数，度量标称DS轨迹上的最坏情况安全性。作者证明在DS模仿学习设定中，无控制输入使可达问题退化为确定性轨迹，特定稳定性条件将无限时域截断为有限时域，从而得到良定义的值函数，并进一步证明该值函数是标称DS流下无障碍区域的最大前向不变子集，据此提出CBF-on-V方法。

Result: 在五种DS构造（解析、Neural ODE、微分同胚潜空间、LPV-DS、SE(3)）上验证了证书函数，并在Franka机械臂上进行了实验。结果表明调制和几何CBF在正面接近不安全区域时存在鞍点问题，而CBF-on-V完全避免了该缺陷。

Conclusion: 所提出的值函数证书为DS策略提供了可计算且有理论保证的安全认证，CBF-on-V方法在多种DS构造和真实机械臂上有效，克服了现有局部安全策略的碰撞与鞍点问题。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems (DS) are reactive motion policies representing vector fields trained with theoretical guarantees of stability and convergence. To ensure safety during deployment in unknown environments they must be locally reshaped, either through modulation or geometric control barrier function strategies. However, depending on the geometry of the obstacles and the complexity of the DS, these local strategies can lead the system to unavoidable collisions or spurious attractors. In this work, we certify safety with a value function drawn from the notion of backward reachability tube, which measures the worst-case safety along a rollout trajectory of the nominal DS. Usually, such a value function is intractable for a controlled system due to curse of dimensionality.
