We show that in the DS-based learning-from-demonstration setting, the absence of a control input collapses the reachability problem to a deterministic rollout, and the presence of certain stability conditions truncates the infinite horizon to a finite one, resulting in a well-defined value function. We further show that the value function we devised is the maximal forward-invariant subset of the obstaclefree region for the nominal DS flow. The application of this certificate function is validated across five DS constructions - analytical, Neural ODE, diffeomorphic latent space, LPV-DS, SE(3)and validate it on a Franka manipulator. Modulation and geometric CBFs also suffer from saddle point in cases of headon approach towards an unsafe zone. We show that CBF-on-V avoids this pitfall entirely.

</details>


### [85] [Embodiment-aware control by inference over the operator: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38437)
*Sara Falcone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"通用具身引擎"(UEE),从隐式注视与瞳孔信号推断操作者具身感及视觉-本体感觉权重,以离散主动推理选择有界设备设置;仿真中多数操作者半分钟内找到合适设置,接近oracle,且收益来自对操作者的贝叶斯推断与显式偏好而非主动推理的信息寻求项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作系统按信道保真度调优,而操作者是否把设备体验为身体一部分(具身感SoE)只在事后用问卷测量。预测加工理论主张通过减少预测与反馈的失配来控制设备,但预测不可观测,且仅惩罚失配的目标可通过"取消反馈"来最小化,因此需要能在线推断具身感的控制框架。

Method: 将问题建模为离散主动推理智能体:利用隐式注视与瞳孔信号及任务结果,在线推断操作者的具身感与视觉-本体感觉线索权重;在显式偏好约束下选择有界设备设置。在300个异构合成操作者上进行仿真,并与固定设置、无模型bandit、同推断的期望效用控制器、朴素预测误差最小化器等基线对比。

Result: UEE在多数操作者上半分钟内找到合适设置,且早于识别其确切类型;后半程接近oracle(具身感1.68 vs 最佳固定设置0.73,0-2量表)。不读取操作者的自适应与固定控制相当,无模型bandit更差,同推断的期望效用控制器表现完全相同;朴素预测误差最小化器按目标所述抑制反馈,任务成功率降至0.74(对照0.90)。收益在模型族外操作者上缩小但仍存在,随群体多样性增大而增大;当信任无信息信号或线索权重中途变化而未建模时收益消失。

Conclusion: 具身感知控制的关键在于对操作者的贝叶斯推断与显式偏好表达,而非主动推理的信息寻求项;预测误差最小化会导致抑制反馈的失败模式。后续以人为对象的研究须先建立两点:经过校准的信号,以及关于变化(线索权重漂移)的模型。

Abstract: Teleoperation systems are tuned for channel fidelity, while whether the operator experiences the device as part of the body, the Sense of Embodiment (SoE), is measured only afterwards, by questionnaire. Predictive-processing accounts suggest controlling devices to reduce the mismatch between the operator's predictions and the returned feedback, but those predictions are unobservable, and an objective that only penalizes mismatch is minimized by removing feedback. We formulate an embodiment-aware controller, the Universal Embodiment Engine (UEE), that infers the operator's embodiment and visuo-proprioceptive cue weighting from implicit gaze and pupil signals and task outcome, and chooses bounded device settings under explicit preferences, cast as a discrete Active Inference agent. In simulations with 300 heterogeneous synthetic operators, the UEE found the suitable setting within half a minute for most operators, before identifying their exact type, and came close to an oracle in the second half of the session (embodiment 1.68 against 0.73 for the best fixed setting, on a 0-2 scale). Adapting without reading the operator did no better than fixed control, and model-free bandits did worse, whereas an expected-utility controller with the same inference did exactly as well: the benefit comes from Bayesian inference over the operator with explicit preferences, not from the information-seeking term of Active Inference. A naive prediction-error minimizer withheld feedback, as its objective implies, and lost task success (0.74 vs 0.90). The benefit shrank but persisted for operators outside the controller's model family, grew with the variety of the population, and vanished when the controller trusted an uninformative signal or when cue weighting changed mid-session without being modeled. These failures show what studies with people must establish first: calibrated signals and a model of change.

</details>


### [86] [BIND: Binding 3D Robot Actions to 2D Image Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38443)
*Cameron Smith,Arsh Tangri,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang,Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIND 是一种新的视觉运动机器人动作表示方法，将 3D 机器人动作与对应的 2D 图像特征绑定，通过相机几何而非学习来建立动作-特征关系，在少样本和分布外场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人策略的动作头通常是从预训练视觉编码器输出的单一全局特征向量进行 MLP 回归。这种全局形式要求策略网络仅从演示中自行发现机器人动作与图像特征之间的对应关系。结果是，尽管现代图像特征具备语义描述性、空间鲁棒性和多视角一致性，但基于它们构建的策略对相机视角和物体位置的细微变化很脆弱，且数据效率出人意料地低。

Method: BIND 通过相机几何而非学习来提供动作-特征关系：它离散化候选末端执行器位置的体积，将每个候选位置与每个相机视角中其投影处的预训练特征关联，并通过评分每个候选的位置和图像绑定特征组合来选择动作。

Result: 在真实机器人上，研究了数据效率、对未见物体位置和相机视角的分布外鲁棒性，以及一般长时程任务执行和灵巧性。BIND 高度数据高效且鲁棒：在仅有 5 个演示的任务上达到近乎完美的成功率，并在相机视角剧烈变化和留出物体位置下优雅退化，而坐标回归基线完全失败。

Conclusion: BIND 通过将 3D 动作绑定到 2D 图像特征，克服了全局特征回归策略的脆弱性和低数据效率问题，在真实机器人上实现了极高的数据效率和分布外鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce BIND, a new action representation for visuomotor robot policies that binds 3D robot actions to their corresponding 2D image features, yielding strong data efficiency gains and robustness to out-of-distribution object positions and camera viewpoints. The action heads of current robot policies are typically formulated as an MLP regression from a single global feature vector produced by a pre-trained vision encoder. This global formulation requires the policy network to discover, from demonstrations alone, the relationship between target robot actions and the image features they project onto. The consequence is that although modern image features are semantically descriptive, spatially robust, and even multiview-consistent, the policies built on them are brittle to subtle changes in camera viewpoint and object placement--and surprisingly data-inefficient. BIND closes this gap by supplying the action-feature relationship through camera geometry rather than learning: it discretizes a volume of candidate end effector positions, attaches each candidate to the pre-trained features at its projection in each camera view, and selects actions by scoring each candidate's position and image-bound feature combination. On a real robot, we study data efficiency and out-of-distribution robustness to unseen object positions and camera viewpoints, as well as general long-horizon task execution and dexterity. We find BIND to be highly data-efficient and robust: it achieves near-perfect success on tasks with as few as 5 demonstrations, and degrades gracefully under steep camera-viewpoint shifts and held-out object positions where coordinate-regression baselines completely fail.

</details>


### [87] [Observability Analysis and Online Calibration of Visual-Inertial-Wheel Odometry for 4WIS4WID Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38462)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Bojan Šekoranja,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向四轮独立转向驱动（4WIS4WID）移动机器人的视觉-惯性-轮式里程计（VIWO）框架，支持在线标定，并通过可观测性分析验证了所提冗余轮速模型的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4WIS4WID机器人的轮式里程计建模复杂，且轮子内参（如转向偏移）的在线标定与可观测性此前缺乏系统分析。作者希望构建一个能直接利用四轮驱动速度与转向角、并支持在线标定的VIWO框架。

Method: 直接从四个驱动速度与转向角推导2D里程计模型，同时利用所有轮子的纵向滚动约束与横向无滑移约束；提出预积分模型与解析雅可比以支持高效滤波与标定；对线性化VIWO系统进行可观测性分析，刻画退化运动及其激励条件。

Result: 可观测性分析表明：仅驱动模型会使所有转向偏移不可观测，而所提冗余模型恢复了其可观测性；识别出四个标准VINS不可观测方向以及三个与里程计参考系任意放置相关的额外方向；刻画了零偏航角速度、常转向、某轮不滚动等退化运动，并给出固定参考系后剩余13个轮子内参的激励条件。仿真与真机实验验证了系统性能。

Conclusion: 所提冗余VIWO模型配合在线标定，在4WIS4WID机器人上有效恢复转向偏移可观测性，并在仿真与真实实验中表现出良好性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a visual-inertial-wheel odometry (VIWO) framework with online calibration for four-wheel independently steered and driven (4WIS4WID) mobile robots. We derive a 2D odometry model directly from the four driving velocities and steering angles, using both the longitudinal rolling constraints and the lateral no-slip constraints of all wheels. A preintegration model and analytical Jacobians are developed for efficient filtering and calibration. An observability analysis of the linearized VIWO system shows that a drive-only model makes all steering offsets unobservable, whereas the proposed redundant model restores their observability. The analysis also identifies four standard VINS unobservable directions and three additional directions associated with the arbitrary placement of the odometry reference frame. Furthermore, we characterize several degenerate motions, including zero yaw rate, constant steering, and a non-rolling wheel, and derive the corresponding excitation conditions for the thirteen wheel intrinsics that remain after fixing the odometry frame reference. The performance of the proposed system has been demonstrated in both simulation and real-world experiments on a 4WIS4WID mobile robot.

</details>


### [88] [TrafficSignBench: Rule-Centric Closed-Loop Evaluation of Traffic-Sign Compliance in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38463)
*Victoria Smirnova,Viktoriia Zinkovich,Gregorii Bukhtuev,Artem Belyaev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Denis Shepelev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrafficSignBench，一个以交通标志为核心的大规模基准，用于系统且可解释地评估自动驾驶规划器的交通规则合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划器通常使用驾驶得分、到达率和碰撞率等聚合指标评估，未显式衡量交通规则合规性，导致规划器可能在获得高基准分数的同时仍表现出不安全或违规行为，限制了实际部署的适用性。

Method: 该框架结合基于真实地图的仿真以生成逼真道路布局，并采用面向规则的程序化场景生成以实现对欠代表性规则的可扩展且均衡覆盖；实现了对应 34 个交通标志的交通规则，每个标志配有自动规则检查器，用于闭环执行中的违规检测；设计产生 29,000 个多样道路场景和 29 种不同测试场景类型；构建 5,800 个测试场景；并通过显式交通标志约束将现有规划器转化为规则合规的轨迹专家，以生成用于微调的高质量 oracle 轨迹。

Result: 当前自动驾驶规划器尽管在标准评估指标上表现强劲，但交通规则合规性可能较差。

Conclusion: TrafficSignBench 提供了系统、可解释的规则合规评估，并可借助规则约束生成高质量轨迹数据用于微调，从而提升规划器的规则合规性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planners are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics such as driving score, destination rate, and collision rate, which do not explicitly measure compliance with traffic rules. As a result, planners can achieve high benchmark scores while still exhibiting unsafe or illegal behaviors, limiting their applicability to real-world deployment. To address this gap, we introduce TrafficSignBench, a large-scale, traffic sign-centric benchmark for systematic and interpretable evaluation of traffic-rule compliance in autonomous driving. Our framework combines real-map-based simulation for realistic road layouts with rule-targeted procedural scenario generation for scalable and balanced coverage of underrepresented rules. We implement traffic rules corresponding to 34 traffic signs, each equipped with an automatic rule checker for detecting violations during closed-loop execution. This design yields 29,000 diverse road scenes and 29 distinct testing scenario types, enabling controlled evaluation of rule-specific planner behavior. We construct 5,800 testing scenes and demonstrate that current autonomous driving planners can exhibit poor traffic-rule compliance despite strong performance on standard evaluation metrics. To address this limitation, we transform existing planners into rule-compliant trajectory experts via explicit traffic-sign constraints, enabling scalable generation of high-quality oracle trajectories for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [89] [L1-MPPI: L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral for Agile UAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38467)
*Lukáš Kotek,Ondřej Procházka,Vojtěch Vonásek,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出L1自适应模型预测路径积分（L1-MPPI），将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联，用于高速无人机轨迹跟踪，在模型不确定性和外部扰动下仍保持高精度，并在仿真和实机中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于无人机控制的MPPI方法通常未显式建模气动效应、载荷变化，且忽略底层电机控制器动力学，导致在模型不确定和负载变化时跟踪性能下降。

Method: 将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联；在MPPI所用动力学模型中纳入底层飞行控制器与电机动力学，并引入反映底层控制器方法的迭代混合方案。

Result: 仿真与实机均显示跟踪性能提升；在载重增加35%的飞行中，相比纯MPPI的RMSE降低58.61%，相比使用在线质量估计器替代L1增强的MPPI降低38.59%；实机速度最高13.50 m/s，加速度达2.5 g。

Conclusion: L1-MPPI通过L1自适应增强与更完整动力学建模，显著提升高速无人机在未知负载等不确定性下的轨迹跟踪精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work proposes the L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral (L1-MPPI). It cascades L1 adaptive control with the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) to improve tracking of high-speed UAV trajectories. Thanks to the L1augmentation, the tracking remains accurate even under model uncertainties and external disturbances, such as an additional payload or a mismatch in the modeled aerodynamic drag. In contrast to existing MPPI approaches for UAV control that do not explicitly model aerodynamic effects, varying payloads, and typically neglect the dynamics of low-level motor controllers, our L1-MPPI approach enhances the dynamic model used in the MPPI by incorporating the low-level flight controller and motor dynamics, as well as an iterative mixing scheme that reflects the approach of the low-level controller. The proposed method demonstrates improved tracking performance in both simulation and the real world, even when the UAV is subjected to an unknown payload. In flight with 35% mass increase, our approach lowers the RMSE by 58.61% with respect to plain MPPI. Compared to the same MPPI using an online mass estimator in place of the L1 augmentation, the RMSE is lower by 38.59%. During the real-world experiments the UAV reaches speeds up to 13.50 m/s and accelerations up to 2.5 g.

</details>


### [90] [Diffusion-2BC: Hybrid Diffusion and Regression Training for Offline Behavior Cloning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38472)
*Bruno Maciel Machado,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Diffusion-2BC，通过在共享视觉编码器上结合扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失，提升自动驾驶扩散策略的闭环可靠性，同时保留多模态动作预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的均方误差回归难以匹配"同一观测对应多个有效动作"的演示数据；扩散策略虽能建模条件多模态动作分布，但在有限数据下视觉特征与控制联合学习时闭环表现可能不稳定。

Method: 在共享视觉编码器上同时优化扩散去噪目标与辅助的确定性行为克隆损失；辅助分支仅用于训练，推理阶段仍采用扩散生成。

Result: 在 Claw 环境中，相比扩散行为克隆基线降低约 10% 的平均掩码距离误差，相比标准确定性行为克隆降低约 85%；在无路线 CARLA 中，Town01 与 Town02 下终止前行驶距离均显著超过两个基线；定性 rollout 显示出不同路线选择，验证多模态行为。

Conclusion: 辅助回归信号可在保持受控基准中多模态预测能力的同时，提升扩散行为克隆的闭环可靠性。

Abstract: Behavior cloning provides an offline route to autonomous-driving policy learning, but mean-squared-error regression is poorly matched to demonstrations in which one observation admits several valid actions. Diffusion policies can represent conditional multimodal action distributions, yet their closed-loop performance may be unstable when visual features and control are learned from limited data. This paper presents Diffusion-2BC, which combines a diffusion denoising objective with an auxiliary deterministic behavior-cloning loss over a shared visual encoder. The auxiliary branch is used only during training; inference remains diffusion-based. The proposed method is evaluated in the controlled Claw environment and in bird's-eye-view CARLA navigation, including route-conditioned driving, route-free navigation through multiple intersections, and cross-map evaluation from Town01 to Town02. In the Claw task, Diffusion-2BC reduced the mean mask-distance error by approximately 10% relative to a diffusion-based behavior-cloning baseline and by 85% relative to standard deterministic behavior cloning. In route-free CARLA, Diffusion-2BC traveled substantially farther before termination under the evaluation protocol than both baselines in Town01 and Town02. Additional qualitative rollouts revealed distinct route choices, showing the multimodal behavior of the proposed diffusion-based agent. The results indicate that an auxiliary regression signal can improve the closed-loop reliability of diffusion behavior cloning while preserving multimodal prediction in the controlled benchmark.

</details>


### [91] [CADeT: Causal-Aware Deformation Transmission for Indirect Robotic Manipulation of Soft Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38483)
*Junlei Hu,Dominic Jones,Pietro Valdastri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CADeT因果感知变形传递框架，结合结构因果模型与主动感知，在线推断潜在传递模式并估计状态相关的黏附雅可比矩阵，实现机器人辅助微创手术中深层组织的间接形变控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助微创手术中，机器人无法直接接触深层可变形解剖结构，中间组织会对形变传递产生空间滤波作用，导致间接操控困难。被动观测可能产生歧义，因为解耦模式与被阻断模式可能给出相似的形变响应，难以区分。

Method: 将结构因果模型（SCM）与主动感知相结合，构建因果感知变形传递框架CADeT。正常操控时，控制动作用于更新模式信念；当歧义持续存在时，选择一个额外的探测动作以提高模式可区分性。将模式信念与学习到的雅可比矩阵嵌入信念感知模型预测控制器，用于间接目标形状控制。

Result: 在仿真以及da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上，使用体模和离体猪组织进行验证，结果显示该方法相比评估的无模型与基于模型基线具有更高的模式识别准确率和更快的形状误差收敛速度。

Conclusion: 在评估条件下，主动感知能够提升模式识别能力以及间接形变控制性能。

Abstract: Indirect manipulation of deep-seated deformable anatomy inaccessible to the robot is challenging in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS) because intervening tissues spatially filter deformation transmission. Passive observations can be ambiguous because the Decoupled and Blocked modes may produce similar motion responses. We propose CADeT, a causal-aware deformation transmission framework that integrates structural causal model (SCM) with active sensing to infer a latent transmission mode and estimate a state-dependent adhesion Jacobian online. During normal manipulation, control actions update the mode belief; when ambiguity persists, an additional probing action is selected to improve mode distinguishability. The mode belief and learned Jacobian are incorporated into a belief-aware model predictive controller for indirect target-shape control. Validation in simulation and on the da Vinci research kit (dVRK), using phantom and ex vivo porcine tissues, shows higher mode-identification accuracy and faster shape-error convergence than the evaluated model-free and model-based baselines. These results show that active sensing improves mode identification and indirect deformation control under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [92] [What to Attend, What to Keep: Skill-Conditioned Visuotactile Representation with Progress-Guided Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38494)
*Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Seungjae Lee,Eadom Dessalene,Shanthosh Raaj Mohanram Mageswari,Soroush Etemad,Furong Huang,Cornelia Fermüller,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种技能条件化的多模态表征，让当前技能决定视觉与触觉的融合方式，并借助稀疏事件记忆保留最近K个技能的关键观测，从而显著提升接触丰富任务中的进度估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中视觉与触觉的重要性随阶段变化：视觉引导接近，触觉随时间演化决定抓取、对齐与接触。然而现有多模态策略通常采用固定的时间上下文与融合策略，忽视了不同技能对各模态贡献的差异化需求，因此需要研究在基础技能层面如何组合视觉与触觉。

Method: 提出技能条件化表征：由查询到的技能作为条件，对模态特定的短期观测token进行融合，同时关注一个稀疏事件记忆，该记忆保留最近K个已执行技能的末端观测。在三个接触丰富任务上以技能进度估计进行评估。

Result: 相比微调的SOTA进度模型，滑移检测延迟降低87%；相比仅视觉的消融，扭转完成检测延迟降低67.5%；在盲搜索任务中通过稀疏事件记忆使进度误差降低92%。增益恰好集中在由接触或任务历史定义完成状态之处。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测形成（observation formation）而非仅策略架构，是多模态表征中的核心挑战；技能条件化融合与稀疏事件记忆能有效利用各模态在不同技能阶段的贡献。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation integrates vision, touch, and language, whose importance shifts across stages: vision guides reaching, while touch, through its evolution over time, decides grasping, alignment, and contact. Yet existing multi-modal manipulation policies typically use fixed temporal contexts and fusion strategies, despite shifts in what each modality contributes across different skills. We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: http://what-to-attend-what-to-keep.github.io/

</details>


### [93] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个开源BlueROV2平台，集成机载视觉与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于受限环境下的自主导航与对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人在受限环境中作业需要稳健的感知、估计与控制能力，而现有平台在机载视觉与NMPC结合、可复现开发环境方面仍有不足。

Method: 平台集成NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX与Intel RealSense D435i立体相机于模块化耐压舱；通过水下标定立体深度与实时目标检测获取邻近BlueROV2的相对位置；采用四元数估计器融合外部位姿与惯性测量；基于六自由度非线性模型设计NMPC控制器跟踪导航与对接轨迹；并提供开源PX4 SITL、Gazebo仿真、多机器人仿真工具及低成本物理对接站。

Result: 通过实验评估了水下感知、机载计算性能、状态估计、轨迹跟踪与自主对接等能力。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为受限环境下水下机器人的自主导航与对接研究提供了集成化、可复现的软硬件与仿真基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [94] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文尝试在不重新训练网络权重的情况下，将二维仿真中共同演化出的通信协议直接迁移到三维物理环境，结果迁移仅部分成功且不对称，表明迁移成效还依赖原有的生态与导航条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验涌现通信协议能否从仿真直接迁移到真实物理机器人，以及信号之外的物理导航约束对迁移成败的影响。

Method: 两台e-puck型机器人由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，执行带社会信号的觅食任务；在感觉—运动转换层做了三处修正（含依据残差权重可测不对称性校准的饥饿项）；并额外在通信通道中加入显式方向信息做对比实验。

Result: 迁移部分且不对称：30个测试种子中仅一个智能体有一次到达食物源，另一个从未到达；加入方向信息可改变接收方轨迹并在若干具体情形下有所改善，但仍无法使第二个智能体到达食物源。

Conclusion: 涌现通信的成功迁移不仅取决于信号传递过程本身的保持，还取决于协议演化时所处的生态与导航条件能否被一并保留。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [95] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估了GPT-6 Astra作为通用具身策略的能力，在六大领域测试其直接控制、与学习策略协作及反馈适应能力，发现其在任务决策上有用但物理控制可靠性不足，且推理延迟严重制约实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望评估GPT-6 Astra能否从高层规划扩展到生成数值化机器人动作，从而作为通用具身策略使用。

Method: 在六大领域（夹爪操作、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动、人形全身操作）开展综合评测，涵盖直接控制、与学习策略协作及反馈驱动适应三种模式，并统计推理延迟与token消耗。

Result: 夹爪操作中混合控制达48%（RoboDojo子集）；灵巧操作混合控制50%（DexJoCo），直接控制难以协调手指接触；移动操作38.7%（RoboCasa365）；导航中RxR指令跟随92%、HM3D物体搜索82%，但搜索绕路严重；运动任务五次尝试均未到达目标；人形全身操作在30个HumanoidBench任务中13个超越基线。推理延迟高：30秒运动需250次模型调用，平均每次39.86秒，期间物理暂停。

Conclusion: GPT-6 Astra在有意义的任务决策与可靠的物理控制之间存在明显差距，推理延迟也进一步限制了实际控制应用。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra exhibits a remarkable ability to generate numerical robot actions, extending its role beyond high-level planning. To assess Astra's capabilities as general-purpose embodied policies, we conduct comprehensive evaluations across six domains, examining direct control, cooperation with learned policies, and feedback-driven adaptation. In gripper manipulation, Astra can correct task targets and prepare contact conditions for subsequent policy execution; hybrid control with π0.5 achieves 48% success on the evaluated RoboDojo subset. In dexterous manipulation, hybrid control achieves 50% success in ten experience-guided DexJoCo trials, while direct in-hand control struggles to coordinate finger contacts. In mobile manipulation, hybrid control reaches 38.7% success on the evaluated RoboCasa365. In navigation, Astra leads our local comparisons, reaching 92% success on RxR instruction following and 82% on HM3D object search, although search incurs substantial detours. In locomotion, dense motion-reference generation remains unreliable: none of five sequential attempts on a single obstacle course reaches the goal, despite improvements in stability and forward progress. In humanoid loco-manipulation, Astra exceeds baseline methods on 13 of 30 HumanoidBench tasks with pretrained whole-body controllers. These findings reveal a gap between useful task decisions and reliable physical control. Inference latency further constrains practical control: across 50 RoboDojo instances per condition, policy-assisted and direct control consume 624.8 million and 1.132 billion tokens. A 30-second locomotion run requires 250 model calls averaging 39.86 seconds each, with physics paused during inference.

</details>


### [96] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of a Driving VLA to Class 8 Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38570)
*Satyajeet Das,Aaron Buxbaum,Niels Joubert,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"先适应后引导"策略，将乘用车VLA模型迁移到Class 8卡车驾驶，通过定向微调加流速度引导模块显著降低轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Class 8卡车在几何、动力学和操控要求上与乘用车不同，为乘用车训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型难以直接迁移到卡车，尤其在事故现场和施工区等非结构化场景中表现不佳。

Method: 采用"adapt-then-steer"两阶段策略：适应阶段以NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5为基础模型，仅微调其动作生成模块，使用几百个真实施工和事故相关高速场景；引导阶段提出Flow Velocity Steering（FVS），这是一个紧凑的、以流时间为条件的残差模块，在保持适应后VLA固定的情况下，为动作空间流速度添加学习到的修正量，从而在每个生成步更新动作序列。

Result: 在场景不相交的留出集开环评估中，定向微调使6.4秒全时域的单候选平均位移误差（ADE）和最终位移误差（FDE）相比基础模型减半以上；FVS进一步将微调模型的全时域ADE和FDE分别降低13.9%和16.5%。在相同数据预算下，定向监督比通用卡车驾驶监督的全时域ADE低19-26%，且定向模型与用约65倍通用卡车驾驶场景微调的模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持"adapt-then-steer"策略可实现面向Class 8卡车的数据高效车辆域迁移，在少量定向数据下即可取得竞争力强的驾驶轨迹预测性能。

Abstract: Class 8 trucks differ from passenger cars in geometry, dynamics, and maneuvering requirements. As a result, vision-language-action (VLA) models trained for passenger vehicles do not readily transfer to Class 8 trucks, particularly in unstructured scenarios such as accident scenes and construction zones. Rather than training a truck-driving VLA from scratch, we propose an adapt-then-steer strategy that adapts an off-the-shelf VLA to generate trajectories for Class-8 trucks in these challenging scenarios. In the adapt stage, we use NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 as the base model, fine-tuning only its action-generation stack on a few hundred real-world construction and accident-related highway scenarios. In the steer stage, we introduce Flow Velocity Steering (FVS) to further refine the model's predictions while holding the adapted VLA fixed. FVS is a compact, flow-time-conditioned residual module that adds learned corrections to the action-space flow velocity used to update the action sequence at each generation step. In open-loop evaluation on a scenario-disjoint held-out set, targeted fine-tuning more than halves single-candidate average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) over the entire 6.4 s horizon compared to the base model. Using the same targeted demonstrations, FVS further reduces the fine-tuned model's full-horizon ADE and FDE by 13.9% and 16.5%, respectively. At matched data budgets, targeted supervision yields 19-26% lower full-horizon ADE than general truck-driving supervision, while the targeted model remains competitive with a model fine-tuned on approximately 65 times as many general truck-driving scenarios. These results support adapt-then-steer for data-efficient vehicle-domain transfer to Class 8 trucks. Our project website is available at https://truckvla.github.io.

</details>


### [97] [Drone Soccer: Learning to Manipulate with Multicopter Downwash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38588)
*Neelay Joglekar,Bavin Saravanan,Yutong Wang,Varun Kandiyappan,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探索利用旋翼无人机桨叶产生的下洗气流(downwash)作为操控工具，而非将其视为干扰，通过无人机足球任务验证了基于下洗的操控可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼无人机主要用于感知类任务(如建图与探索)，近期研究转向无人机操控器(UAM)以执行移动操控任务。然而，桨叶产生的下洗气流会影响操控性能，现有先进UAM要么忽略下洗，要么将其视为干扰。作者提出反问：能否主动将下洗气流用作操控工具？

Method: 设计了一个"无人机足球"任务来探究基于下洗的操控可行性。开发了一个简化的下洗动力学模型，并基于该模型训练强化学习(RL)策略，使无人机能够带球(运球)。

Result: 训练得到的RL策略成功实现了带球行为，并且该策略能够迁移到真实世界部署中，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作为多旋翼无人机的新型操控能力提供了关键见解，证明下洗气流可被主动用作操控工具，开辟了新的操控可能性。

Abstract: Although multicopter drones are traditionally designed for "perception-only" tasks, like mapping and exploration, recent work has sought to develop Unmanned Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) to solve mobile manipulation tasks. Aerial manipulation performance can be impacted by "downwash," the airflow produced by propellers, but current state-of-the-art UAMs either ignore downwash or treat it as a disturbance. Instead, is it possible to actively use downwash as a tool during manipulation? We design a drone soccer task to explore the feasibility of downwash-based manipulation. Specifically, we develop a simplified downwash dynamics model which we use to train an RL policy to dribble a soccer ball. We further demonstrate that our policy transfers to real world deployment. This work provides key insights into novel manipulation capabilities for multicopters.

</details>


### [98] [FAST-Sync: Fast Group Synchronization for any Matrix Lie Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38594)
*Shane Holmes,Yiran Luo,Firat Taxpulat,David M. Rosen,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fast-Sync，一种适用于任意矩阵李群的快速线性近似群同步（GS）初始化方法，通过利用Kronecker积结构和同步图拓扑提升速度、可扩展性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群同步问题广泛存在于机器人状态估计与计算机视觉任务（如3D视觉、机器人建图、惯性导航、分子重建）中，但通常具有高维、非凸的特点，难以直接求解。因此需要一个高质量的初始化方法，用于启动局部流形优化器或可认证的全局方法。

Method: 提出Fast-Sync，一种快速线性近似方法。它把弦初始化（chordal initialization）推广到任意矩阵李群；并引入两项关键算法增强：利用问题数据矩阵中的Kronecker积结构，以及利用同步图的拓扑结构，以提升速度、可扩展性和精度。

Result: 在多个群同步任务的实验评估中，Fast-Sync能提供高质量初始化，使局部优化器高效地恢复全局最优的GS解，即使在较大的测量噪声下也能达到较高的成功率。

Conclusion: Fast-Sync可作为群同步问题的有效初始化方案，兼容局部流形优化器和可认证全局方法，在速度、可扩展性和精度上均有提升，并在多类GS任务中表现出高成功率。

Abstract: Group synchronization (GS) is the problem of estimating a set of $N$ unknown elements $g_1,\ldots, g_N \in \mathcal{G}$ in a group $\mathcal{G}$, given noisy measurements of a subset of their pairwise ratios $g_i^{-1} g_j$. GS problems lie at the core of many state estimation tasks in robotics and computer vision, including 3D vision, robotic mapping, inertial navigation, and molecular reconstruction. Unfortunately, GS problems are typically both high-dimensional and non-convex, and therefore hard to solve in general. In this paper, we present Fast-Sync, a fast linear approximation method for GS that is suitable for initializing local manifold-based optimizers or certifiable global methods. Our approach generalizes chordal initialization to arbitrary matrix Lie groups, and additionally proposes two new key algorithmic enhancements: we show how to exploit both the Kronecker-product structure in the problem data matrix and the topology of the synchronization graph to improve speed, scalability, and accuracy. Experimental evaluation across several GS tasks demonstrates that Fast-Sync provides high-quality initializations that enable local optimizers to efficiently recover globally optimal GS solutions, achieving high success rates even with considerable measurement noise.

</details>


### [99] [Correcting WHERE, Preserving HOW: Compositional Generalization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Referential Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38616)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Xinpeng Li,Chaoda Song,Mohsen Hariri,Debargha Ganguly,Wang Yang,Kai Ye,Bryce Grant,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 ReGuide 包装器，通过语义与几何重绑定引导机械臂进入示范支持的参考物配置，从而在组合泛化场景下显著提升 VLA 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽能灵活生成动作，但训练数据多样性不足，导致其依赖视觉捷径，将动作与任务无关的视觉特征关联，难以实现元素重组（组合泛化）。现有方法依赖任务相关感知或数据多样化，缺乏对未见重组的显式机制，且需针对特定骨干网络修改并重新训练。

Method: 观察到重组下 VLA 全局定位失败但仍保留局部操作技能，据此提出 Referential Guidance（ReGuide）：一种免训练包装器，利用定位模块给出的物体位姿，结合语义与几何重绑定，将末端执行器引导至指令所指参考物的示范支持配置，之后冻结策略继续执行。

Result: 在多种 VLA 骨干网络的仿真实验以及真实机器人实验中，ReGuide 在组合偏移下成功率分别提升最高 56.8 和 75.0 个百分点，同时保持标准任务性能。

Conclusion: ReGuide 作为一种无需训练、无需修改骨干网络的通用包装器，有效缓解视觉捷径问题，实现组合泛化增强，并具备跨模型与真实场景的适用性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable flexible action generation, their generalization across diverse environmental elements, including manipulated objects, destinations, and backgrounds, is limited by the lack of diversity in robotic training data. Trained end-to-end on such data, VLAs tend to exploit visual shortcuts, associating actions with task-irrelevant visual features rather than the intended task semantics. These shortcuts block recomposition of elements already seen by the policy, that is, compositional generalization. Existing approaches mitigate such entanglement through task-relevant perception or targeted data diversification, but offer no explicit mechanism for unseen recomposition and require backbone-specific modifications with retraining. We observe that under such recomposition, VLAs often fail at global grounding while retaining local manipulation skills that recover near the correct target in familiar configurations. Therefore, we propose Referential Guidance (ReGuide), a training-free wrapper that, given object poses from a grounding module, combines semantic and geometric rebinding to guide the end-effector into demonstration-supported configurations of the instructed referent, where the frozen policy can resume execution. Experiments in simulation across multiple VLA backbones as well as on a real robot show that ReGuide improves success rates under compositional shifts by up to 56.8 and 75.0 percentage points, respectively, while preserving standard-task performance.

</details>


### [100] [Dense Temporal Motion Retargeting for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38617)
*Jaeryeong Kim,Taerim Yoon,Jin Cheng,Sungjoon Choi,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出密集时空运动重定向（DTMR），在单个优化中联合优化时序与控制，通过GPU并行采样MPC求解，使动作仅在需要处进行时序变形，在动态动作上优于基线并能迁移到真实人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类与动物动作与机器人存在形态差异，动作需适配机器人的动力学特性；尤其跳跃等动态动作的时序与控制相互依赖，必须精细调整。

Method: 提出DTMR，在单一优化中联合优化时序与控制，时序按每个控制步进行密集调整；采用基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）在GPU上并行求解。

Result: 在两小时人类动作和四种人形机器人上与基线对比，DTMR表现更优，尤其在动态动作上；时序变形越多，重定向越精确；与优化时序维度的基线相比，在相同变形预算下更精确且快约19倍；所训练的策略可迁移到真实人形机器人。

Conclusion: DTMR可高效精确地实现跨形态全身运动重定向，并支持从参考动作学习可迁移到真实机器人的策略。

Abstract: Legged robots can learn expressive whole-body skills from the motions of humans and animals. Due to the morphology gap between the source and the robot, however, the motion must be tailored to the dynamic properties of the robot. In particular, dynamic motions such as a jump require careful adjustment, since their timing and control are interdependent. We propose dense temporal motion retargeting (DTMR), which jointly optimizes timing and control within a single optimization, where dense means that the timing is adjusted for every control step. This dense formulation enables DTMR to deform only the parts of the motion that need a change in timing. The problem is solved with sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) in parallel on a GPU. We evaluate DTMR against baselines on two hours of human motion with four humanoid robots, where the results show that DTMR outperforms baseline methods, particularly on dynamic motions. We also show that allowing more temporal deformation yields more precise retargeting. We further compare DTMR with a baseline that optimizes the temporal dimension, where the result shows that DTMR retargets more precisely under the same deformation budget while being ~19x faster. Lastly, policies trained on our references transfer to a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [101] [Yggdrasil: a Layer-First 3D Scene Graph for Real-Time Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38640)
*Arshia Akhavan,Ermanno Bartoli,Afnan Algharbi,Alireza Hoseinpur,Iolanda Leite,Bryan Donyanavard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Yggdrasil，首个同时针对生成与消费效率优化的 3D 场景图，采用层级化的通用节点-边-层结构，在保持既有表征能力的同时原生支持下游任务的位置与语义查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于 3D 场景图的生成，却忽视了对图的消费优化，导致最先进的感知流程需要绕过自身场景图，并付出不符合实时感知预算的延迟代价。

Method: Yggdrasil 是一种层优先的层级图结构，由通用节点、边和层构成，可表达已有流程产出的各类表征（室内/室外、扁平/层级），并原生回答下游任务提出的位置与语义查询。

Result: 相比已发表的 DSG 基线，Yggdrasil 的查询速度最高快 121 倍，所有测量查询耗时在 2 至 127 微秒之间，落在 200 微秒关键帧预算内三到五个数量级，且在工作站与嵌入式设备上均成立。在三条已发表流程（人体轨迹预测、物体目标导航、人感知运动规划）中集成后，可消除其场景图处理高达 99% 的时间。

Conclusion: Yggdrasil 兼顾生成与消费效率，显著提升查询速度并满足实时预算，具备良好的跨平台与跨任务适用性，实现、基准测试框架及三个集成方案均已开源。

Abstract: Robotic agents use 3D scene graphs (3DSG) to perform tasks ranging from scene understanding to scene interaction. Although an extensive body of work addresses scene graph generation, little attention has been paid to optimizing the graph for consumption, which leaves state-of-the-art perception pipelines to work around their own scene graph and to pay a latency cost that does not fit the real-time budget a perception loop runs on.
  We present Yggdrasil, the first 3D scene graph designed to be efficient for both generation and consumption: a layer-first hierarchical graph built from generic nodes, edges, and layers, which expresses the representations existing pipelines already produce, indoor or outdoor, flat or hierarchical, while natively answering the positional and semantic queries downstream tasks issue.
  Against a published DSG baseline, Yggdrasil answers queries up to $121\times$ faster, and every query we measure falls between 2 and 127 microseconds, three to five orders of magnitude inside the 200 microsecond keyframe budget a 3DSG consumer lives in, on both a workstation and embedded class device. We integrate Yggdrasil into three published pipelines spanning human trajectory prediction, object-goal navigation, and human-aware motion planning, where it removes up to 99% of the time each spends on its scene graph. The implementation, benchmark harness, and all three integrations are available online.

</details>


### [102] [TERRA: Terrain-Aware Reconstruction, Retargeting and Control for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38653)
*Merkourios Simos,Chengkun Li,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA是一个端到端流程，可从无场景的运动数据中恢复支撑地形并完成肌肉驱动模型的运动重定向与控制，实现复杂非平坦地形上的行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌肉驱动代理的运动复现能力多局限于平地，主要原因是运动数据集缺少对齐的地形几何信息，且将地形交互重定向到复杂肌骨模型上十分困难。

Method: TERRA仅从运动学轨迹出发，结合地形先验、估计的接触以及负自由空间证据来恢复任务相关的支撑几何；在重定向过程中引入解剖学约束、肌腱连续性约束和接触约束；基于五个数据集生成运动-地形配对数据。

Result: 在重建、重定向和留出跟踪基准上，TERRA提升了地形精度，显著减少解剖与交互违规，并在各类支撑地形上取得最高的完成率；使用9.4小时多样化运动数据训练出单一肌肉驱动控制策略。

Conclusion: TERRA为从无场景运动数据到复杂非平坦地形上的肌肉驱动运动提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in musculoskeletal modeling and reinforcement learning have enabled muscle-actuated agents to reproduce increasingly complex human motions. Yet these capabilities remain largely confined to flat ground, in part because motion datasets rarely include aligned terrain geometry and because retargeting terrain interactions to complex musculoskeletal bodies is challenging. We present TERRA, an end-to-end pipeline for terrain-aware retargeting and control of musculoskeletal locomotion. From kinematic trajectories alone, TERRA combines terrain priors, estimated contacts, and negative free-space evidence to recover task-relevant support geometry. TERRA further considers anatomical, tendon-continuity, and contact constraints during retargeting. Using the resulting motion-terrain pairs from five datasets, we successfully train a single muscle-actuated control policy on 9.4 hours of diverse locomotion. Across reconstruction, retargeting, and held-out tracking benchmarks, TERRA improves terrain accuracy, sharply reduces anatomical and interaction violations, and achieves the highest observed completion rate over supported terrain families. Overall, TERRA provides a practical route from scene-less motion data to muscle-actuated locomotion over diverse non-flat terrain. Project website: https://cnai.epfl.ch/terra/

</details>


### [103] [Residual Wrench Certification and Margin-Aware Control Synthesis for Aerial Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38707)
*Abhimanyu Khadga,Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种任务相关的框架，用于在多旋翼执行器受限条件下，认证悬停与接触载荷后的残余力螺旋权限，涵盖余量推导、命令投影、增益综合及闭环验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼在悬停及与刚性壁面接触时，执行器受限会导致可用力螺旋权限下降，需要一种可计算的证书来量化并保证残余控制权限，支持任务可行的分配与跟踪设计。

Method: 基于凸几何推导预设凸储备（欧氏球与加权椭球）的有符号余量；由执行器内点界获得可计算的储备证书以支持松弛最大化分配；提出命令投影到收紧可行集以保留储备；将可用储备与结构化增益综合关联，认证满足执行器限制的局部跟踪域。

Result: 在给定任务与形态族下，优化八旋翼在等总推力时余量大于优化六旋翼。闭环仿真中，固定几何八旋翼持续刚性壁接触，可变倾转四旋翼在匹配安装推力下，其局部证书对给定任务族允许更高的归一化推力上限；超出可实现边界时，分别观察到八旋翼的旋翼推力限制与四旋翼的倾转伺服限制导致的权限损失。

Conclusion: 该框架可定量认证受限多旋翼的残余力螺旋权限，并通过命令投影与增益综合保证局部跟踪域内的标称指数收敛与鲁棒最终有界性，为接触任务下的形态选择与分配设计提供依据。

Abstract: We develop a task-relative framework for certifying residual wrench authority after hover and contact loading in multirotors with bounded actuators. Using convex geometry, we derive signed margins for prescribed convex reserves, including Euclidean balls and weighted ellipsoids. We obtain computable reserve certificates from actuator-interiority bounds to support slack-maximizing allocation. To preserve the required reserve, we propose command projection onto a tightened feasible set. We then connect the available reserve to structured gain synthesis and certify a local tracking region that respects actuator limits. Within this region, we establish nominal exponential convergence and robust ultimate boundedness. For the prescribed task and morphology family, we show that the optimized octarotor attains a larger margin than the optimized hexarotor at equal total thrust. We evaluate the proposed framework in closed-loop simulations of sustained rigid-wall contact using a fixed-geometry octarotor and a variable-tilt quadrotor with matched installed thrust. The quadrotor's local certificate admits a higher normalized push limit for the prescribed task family. In tests beyond the realizability boundaries, we observe the predicted loss of authority through rotor-thrust limits for the octarotor and tilt-servo limits for the quadrotor.

</details>


### [104] [CEER2: Directional and Tunable End-Effector and Root Compliance for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38709)
*Xinyuan Luo,Chunyuan Yang,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向人形机器人全身运动-操作任务的柔顺框架，将末端执行器方向性可调柔顺与可选根部柔顺结合，用分层强化学习控制器实现外力响应，并通过仿真与真机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人虽能跟踪复杂全身运动，但在与人类或环境发生物理接触时面临新挑战：既要响应外力，又要保持任务所需的运动。这种响应需求在不同方向上可能不同（如末端执行器某方向顺应接触力、另一方向保持运动精度；机器人本体可能抵抗外力或随外力移动），现有方法难以同时满足方向性与可调性的柔顺需求。

Method: 提出一种用于人形机器人locomotion-manipulation的柔顺框架，结合两类机制：（1）末端执行器的方向性、可在线调节的柔顺；（2）可选择的根部柔顺，用于外力拒斥或力跟随。采用分层强化学习控制器：高层输出EE与根部命令，对固定的全身跟踪策略进行调制；交互力则通过本体感知历史进行估计。

Result: 在仿真与真实人形机器人实验中展示了以下能力：方向性刚度控制、在线刚度调整、可区分的根部柔顺行为、柔顺操作，以及协作搬运。

Conclusion: 该框架能够统一实现方向性与可调的末端柔顺及根部柔顺选择，为人类机器人物理交互与协作任务提供了可行的控制方案。

Abstract: Humanoids are increasingly capable of tracking complex whole-body motions, but physical interaction introduces a different challenge. When a robot makes contact with a person or the environment, it needs to respond to external forces while preserving the motion needed for the task. This response can vary across directions in the end-effectors and on the body. For example, an end effector may need to accommodate contact force in one direction while maintaining motion accuracy in another, while the robot body may resist an external force or move with it. We present a compliance framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines directional and tunable end-effector (EE) compliance with selectable root compliance for external force rejection or force following. A hierarchical reinforcement learning controller modulates a fixed whole-body tracking policy through high-level EE and root commands, while interaction forces are estimated from proprioceptive history. Our simulation and real-world experiments on a humanoid demonstrate directional stiffness control, online stiffness adjustment, distinct root compliance, compliant manipulation, and collaborative carrying.

</details>


### [105] [Path-Following Control and Terramechanics Analysis for Planetary Rovers Under Wheel-to-Wheel Traction Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38715)
*Ryuya Matsuoka,Keisuke Takehana,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模型、仅靠减速实现打滑转向星球车路径跟踪的控制策略，在牵引力持续不对称的松散变形地形上抑制侧向漂移和沉陷陷入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学的控制器在路径修正时加速车轮，容易造成滑移-沉陷陷入（slip-sinkage entrapment）；而星球车在松散变形地形上常面临持续的牵引力不对称，需避免此类失效。

Method: 采用无模型控制策略，对最大指令速度设上限；航向修正仅通过选择性减速转弯外侧车轮，使其进入负滑移状态充当机械锚点。使用四轮独立驱动星球车EX1，在左右花纹高度不同的非对称轮配置下进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，仅减速控制在最高0.7 m/s的各种速度区间内均能抑制累积侧向漂移且不引起沉陷。车载多轴传感器直接测力证明，目标减速可实现底盘动态载荷均衡，并完全恢复对侧驱动轮的原生推力能力。

Conclusion: 仅减速、无模型的控制策略可有效实现非对称牵引条件下星球车的路径跟踪，避免沉陷失效，并为底层地面力学机制提供了实证支持。

Abstract: This paper proposes a control strategy for path following that is model-free and relies solely on deceleration for skid-steering planetary rovers navigating deformable loose terrain under continuously imposed traction asymmetry. While conventional controllers that are based on kinematics frequently cause slip-sinkage entrapment by accelerating the wheels during path correction, the proposed approach prevents this failure by setting an upper limit on the maximum commanded velocity. Heading correction is achieved solely through the selective deceleration of the outer wheels, which are located on the outside of the turn, driving them into a negative slip regime to act as a mechanical anchor. The system was evaluated using the four-wheel independent-drive rover EX1 under an asymmetric wheel configuration with different left and right grouser heights that induces significant deviations from the path. Experimental results demonstrate that this deceleration-only control successfully suppresses accumulated lateral drift across various velocity regimes up to 0.7 m/s without causing sinkage. Crucially, direct force measurements from onboard multi-axis sensors provide important empirical evidence of the underlying terramechanics, proving that the targeted deceleration establishes dynamic load equalization across the chassis and completely restores the native thrust capability of the opposite driving wheel.

</details>


### [106] [FlapKAD: A Simulation Dataset of Coupled Wing Kinematics and Aerodynamic Dynamics for Flapping-Wing Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38719)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了FlapKAD——一个包含2000个刚性翼飞行片段、720152个有效时间步的仿真数据集，同步记录双侧翼运动学、气动响应与飞行状态，并基于此构建双向序列预测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器的实验研究与建模受限于大规模数据的稀缺，现有数据无法在时间上对齐翼运动学、气动响应与飞行状态，且存在测量噪声与时间错位问题，尤其在高频扑动时更为严重。

Method: 构建FlapKAD仿真数据集，采用片段化（episode）结构，在同一时钟下同步记录双侧翼运动学、气动响应与飞行状态；基于时间对齐的片段构建统一的双向序列预测基准，包含正向任务（由扑动与扭转角历史预测未来垂直力系数与机体垂直速度）和逆向任务（由响应历史重建未来扑动与扭转角轨迹），并在多预测时域上评测八种代表性时间序列架构。

Result: 基准结果显示模型行为具有方向依赖与时域依赖；扭转角的重建误差系统性地高于扑动角；增加架构复杂度并未带来一致优势。

Conclusion: FlapKAD为研究扑翼飞行器中翼运动学、气动与飞行状态的耦合动力学提供了可复现的数据集与基准。

Abstract: Experimental investigation and modeling of flapping-wing aerial vehicles are limited by the scarcity of large-scale records that temporally align wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states. Existing datasets are often limited in scale and affected by measurement noise and temporal misalignment between rapidly varying wing motion and the associated dynamic response, particularly during high-frequency flapping. We introduce FlapKAD, an episode-structured simulation dataset comprising 2,000 rigid-wing flight episodes and 720,152 valid time steps, with bilateral wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states recorded synchronously within a common clock. FlapKAD supports a unified bidirectional sequence-prediction benchmark constructed from the same temporally aligned episodes. The forward task predicts future vertical force coefficients and body vertical velocity from histories of realized flap and twist angles, whereas the inverse task reconstructs future flap- and twist-angle trajectories from the corresponding response histories. A benchmark of eight representative time-series architectures across multiple prediction horizons reveals direction- and horizon-dependent model behavior, systematically higher reconstruction errors for twist angle than for flap angle, and no consistent advantage from increased architectural complexity. FlapKAD provides a reproducible dataset and benchmark for studying coupled wing-kinematic, aerodynamic, and flight-state dynamics in flapping-wing aerial vehicles.

</details>


### [107] [Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Learning for Forward-Consistent Inverse Flapping-Wing Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38730)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BCS-GRU模型，将有界通道自适应谱残差与门控循环单元结合，用于扑翼飞行器气动力学的逆向运动学预测，并通过前向一致性目标提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器通过行程、偏移和俯仰运动的协调变化调节气动力与力矩，负载依赖瞬时翼型构型及先前运动历史，因此从期望气动轨迹反推合适翼运动学是困难的逆问题。现有序列模型能捕捉时间依赖，谱方法可利用周期性，但不受限的频域增强会干扰时间表征，导致运动学变量与预测时域间修正不一致。

Method: 提出有界通道自适应谱残差门控循环单元（BCS-GRU）：以循环时间预测为主表征，将谱信息限制为可控的、输出特定的修正项；并引入因果对齐的前向一致性目标，通过单独训练并冻结的气动代理模型评估预测运动学。

Result: 在统一 episode 级协议下，BCS-GRU 在逆向预测上优于循环基线和自适应谱基线，且预测时域越长收益越大；前向一致微调进一步改善基于代理的气动一致性，同时保持平均运动学精度。

Conclusion: 受控谱修正与前向一致学习为扑翼气动力学的历史感知逆建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Flapping-wing vehicles regulate aerodynamic forces and moments through coordinated variations in stroke, deviation, and pitch motion. Because the resulting loads depend on both the instantaneous wing configuration and its preceding motion history, recovering suitable wing kinematics from a desired aerodynamic trajectory is a challenging inverse problem. Existing sequence models capture temporal dependencies, while spectral methods can exploit the periodic structure of flapping motion. However, unrestricted frequency-domain augmentation may interfere with temporal representations and produce inconsistent corrections across kinematic variables and prediction horizons. We propose the Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Residual Gated Recurrent Unit (BCS-GRU), which retains recurrent temporal prediction as its primary representation and restricts spectral information to a controlled output-specific correction. We further introduce a causally aligned forward-consistency objective that evaluates predicted kinematics through a separately trained and frozen aerodynamic surrogate. Experiments under a unified episode-level protocol show that BCS-GRU improves inverse prediction over recurrent and adaptive-spectral baselines, with greater benefits at longer prediction horizons. Forward-consistent fine-tuning further improves surrogate-based aerodynamic consistency while maintaining mean kinematic accuracy. These results demonstrate that controlled spectral correction and forward-consistent learning provide an effective framework for history-aware inverse modelling of flapping-wing aerodynamics.

</details>


### [108] [AIfred: Augmented Learning through Functional Robotic Embodiment at the Desk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38737)
*Gregorio Orlando,Milan Groshev,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了 AIfred——一台末端执行器搭载投影仪的桌面机械臂，可将 AI 生成的指导直接投射到手写作业旁，实现"空间共址"的 AI 辅助，并通过用户研究验证其优于屏幕式 AI 工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面手写学习与创作活动依赖手写投入，但现有生成式 AI 工具通过独立屏幕提供指导，造成"用户思考之处"与"辅助出现之处"之间的空间割裂。作者希望消除这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建 AIfred 系统：桌面机械臂 + 末端投影仪，融合工作区感知、上下文感知内容生成与机器人投影，支持数学作业、图像生成和绘画任务。开展用户研究（n = 36），将其与笔记本电脑上的 ChatGPT（GPT-5.6 Luna）进行对比。

Result: 在有辅助时，数学作业中两者表现相当（6.7 vs. 7.3/10，p = .41）；但撤除辅助后，AIfred 的短期学习迁移提升 60%（7.0 vs. 4.4/10，p = .003）。独立的艺术与设计教授在 36 组绘画中有 33 组认为 AIfred 辅助的作品更好。

Conclusion: 空间共址的 AI 辅助对"指导与工作共享同一空间框架"的任务更有益，即辅助出现在用户实际动手操作的位置时，能带来更优的学习迁移与创作效果。

Abstract: Desk-based learning and creative activities benefit from handwritten engagement. However, current generative AI tools deliver guidance through a separate screen, creating a gap between where users think and where assistance appears. To address this, in this work we design AIfred, a desk-based robotic arm with a projector mounted at the end-effector that places AI-generated guidance alongside handwritten work. AIfred combines workspace perception, context-aware content generation, and robot-mediated projection to support math assignments, image generation, and drawing tasks. In a user study (n = 36), we compared AIfred against ChatGPT (GPT-5.6 Luna) running on a laptop. Both tools performed comparably while assistance was available during the math assignment (6.7 vs. 7.3/10, p = .41), but AIfred improved short-term learning transfer by 60% once assistance was withdrawn (7.0 vs. 4.4/10, p = .003). In addition, independent art and design professors ranked drawings produced with AIfred better in 33 of 36 cases. Our findings indicate that spatially co-located AI assistance benefits tasks whose guidance shares a spatial frame with the work.

</details>


### [109] [HALO: Heterogeneous Allocation Via Localized Observations for the Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38760)
*Andrew Meighan,Hyungsub Kim,Or Dantsker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO，一种面向去中心化机器人车队的混合式车辆路径问题（VRP）求解方法，通过将VRP拆分为分配与路由两个部分，结合异构图神经网络与独特消息传递层，实现部分可观测、在线环境下的实时求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车队控制算法多在受控环境下求解集中式路由问题（最多约1000个任务），未考虑去中心化车队的有限观测与通信范围等现实约束，导致难以落地于真实场景。

Method: HALO将VRP分为分配和路由两部分，为动态环境中的机器人提供机载实时解。分配阶段采用异构图神经网络框架，配合独特消息传递层，显式分离空间分布学习与任务-机器人兼容性学习。

Result: 在部分可观测在线VRP变体上，HALO显著优于启发式基线，解质量接近全知离线变体；在传统静态单仓库VRP上，较严格针对静态VRP优化的最先进架构最高提升14.06%；在所有测试中均保持最快执行时间。

Conclusion: HALO虽为部分可观测环境设计，但对观测空间无严格上界，可泛化至传统静态VRP；其快速执行特性凸显了大规模实时部署的潜力。

Abstract: Scalable robotic fleets have become increasingly popular for various applications such as package delivery, warehouse management, and military operations. Prior fleet control algorithms solve centralized routing problems with up to $1{,}000$ tasks in controlled environments, yet they fail to consider realistic constraints such as limited observation and communication ranges typical of decentralized fleets. Thus, deploying existing fleet control algorithms into real-world settings is currently infeasible.
  To tackle this, we propose Heterogeneous Allocation via Localized Observations (HALO) to solve the Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP). HALO is a hybrid method that splits the VRP into allocation and routing portions to provide onboard, real-time solutions to robots in dynamic environments. During the allocation phase, HALO utilizes a heterogeneous graph neural network framework with unique message passing layers to explicitly separate the learning of spatial distributions and task-to-robot compatibility.
  Evaluation results on a partially observable, online variant of the VRP show HALO significantly outperforms the heuristic baseline while maintaining similar solution quality to an all-knowing offline variant of HALO. While HALO is explicitly designed for partially observable environments, it imposes no strict upper bound on the observation space allowing us to test HALO on the traditional static, single-depot VRP. Here, HALO outperforms state-of-the-art architectures strictly optimized for the static variant of the VRP by up to $14.06\%$. Throughout all testing, this framework maintains the quickest execution times which emphasizes its potential for large-scale, real-time deployment.

</details>


### [110] [PhaseSync-Exo: Human Clock Anchored Reference Adaptation for Dynamic Gait Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38824)
*Kaijie Qi,Yuehan Wang,Kaiming Xu,Chong Li,Jiakuo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhaseSync-Exo框架，通过双IMU重建、分解式步态重定向与"人类时钟锚定适配器"（HCA），在仿真中实现外骨骼步态连续相位获取与持续对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人感知型外骨骼行走需同时满足：重建步态、在动态约束下跟踪多样运动、并保持人类自身的时间节律（timing），现有方法难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 1）双IMU的CNN-Transformer步态重建；2）分解式幅值-步频重定向（factorized amplitude-cadence retargeting）与课程训练循环控制；3）人类时钟锚定适配器（HCA），将人类时钟吸引项与机器人相对反馈结合，调整参考速率同时保持前向推进与连续性。

Result: 重建模块在留出记录上MAE为3.24°；冻结跟踪策略完成357次幅频试验中的356次。HCA相较机器人相对校正将人类时钟相位MAE从95.55°降至10.99°；初始相位不一致时相较固定时钟延续从72.02°降至13.90°；相较即时相位重置，瞬态参考跟踪髋RMSE降低22.7%且无参考跳变。420次时序rollout无跌倒。

Conclusion: 仿真结果支持连续相位获取与对独立人类时钟的持续对齐，HCA在不重新训练的前提下带来时序增益。

Abstract: Human-aware exoskeleton walking requires reconstructing gait, tracking diverse motions under dynamic constraints, and preserving human timing. We present PhaseSync-Exo, which combines two-IMU CNN-Transformer reconstruction, factorized amplitude-cadence retargeting with curriculum-trained recurrent control, and a human-clock-anchored adapter (HCA). HCA combines human-clock attraction with robot-relative feedback to adjust reference rate while preserving forward progression and continuity. The reconstruction module achieves a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.24 degrees on a held-out recording, and the frozen tracking policy completes 356 of 357 amplitude-frequency trials. Two complementary dynamic comparisons isolate HCA's timing benefit without retraining. Against robot-relative correction, HCA reduces human-clock phase MAE from 95.55 degrees to 10.99 degrees, limiting reference drift. When human and delivered phases initially differ, it reduces phase MAE from 72.02 degrees to 13.90 degrees versus fixed-clock continuation. Compared with immediate phase reset, HCA reduces transient reference-tracking hip RMSE by 22.7% without reference jumps. All 420 timing rollouts complete without falls. These simulation results support continuous phase acquisition and sustained alignment to an independent human clock.

</details>


### [111] [Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS): Scaling Coral Recruit Monitoring Through Robotics and Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38846)
*Dorian Tsai,Scarlett Raine,Emilio Olivastri,Riki Lamont,Andrew Lui,Timothy Morris,Joshua Esplin,Christopher A. Brunner,F. Mikaela Nordborg,Reginald Wardleworth,Garima Samvedi,Karen Jackel,Matthew Dunbabin,Tobias Fischer,Andrea Severati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出珊瑚培育机器人评估系统CGRAS，结合机器人成像与计算机视觉，自动化监测珊瑚幼苗的生长、存活与空间分布，将监测时间与人力成本降低9.6倍，对Acropora kenti的计数与专家一致性达96.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化是珊瑚礁最大威胁，规模化修复依赖珊瑚大规模养殖（如珊瑚苗圃），而珊瑚播种等修复方式需要对数万个0.5–2mm的宏观幼苗进行高频、一致性监测。传统人工评估劳动强度过高，成为生产瓶颈，因此需要自动化监测技术。

Method: 提出Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS)，集成机器人成像与计算机视觉，自动完成数据采集、多物种珊瑚检测与计数以及健康评估；自动提取珊瑚生长、存活和空间分布指标，为操作人员提供及时反馈以优化生产、培育与部署流程。系统在大型水产养殖设施中基于标准化珊瑚附着瓦片进行验证。

Result: 相较人工监测，CGRAS将时间与人力成本降低9.6倍；对Acropora kenti珊瑚的计数与专家计数一致性达96.4%。

Conclusion: CGRAS可有效缓解珊瑚养殖中的监测瓶颈，实现高效、可扩展的自动化评估，为大规模珊瑚修复提供支持。

Abstract: Climate change is the largest threat to coral reefs, with increasing global impacts accelerating the need for scalable reef restoration technologies. Large-scale reef restoration depends on the mass production of corals, such as through coral aquaculture. Coral seeding with recruits grown in aquaculture facilities is a feasible restoration approach, but effective production requires consistent, high-frequency monitoring of tens of thousands of macroscopic (0.5-2mm diameter) recruits, making conventional manual assessment prohibitively labor-intensive. To address this monitoring bottleneck, we introduce the Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS) which combines robotic imaging and computer vision to automate data acquisition, perform multi-species detection and counting of corals, and evaluate coral health. CGRAS automatically extracts coral growth, survival and spatial distribution metrics, with the aim of providing timely feedback to operators for optimizing production, grow-out and deployment workflow processes. We demonstrate CGRAS in a large aquaculture facility on standardized coral settlement tiles, reducing the time and labor costs by a factor of 9.6 as compared to manual monitoring, whilst achieving 96.4% agreement for Acropora kenti corals relative to expert counts.

</details>


### [112] [Locomotion-Grounded Humanoid Soccer: Task-Gated Reinforcement Learning of a Multi-Directional Kicking Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38852)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Jaehyun Jang,Hwanhee Kim,Kyuwon Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将人形足球系统"倒置"：先训练通用的指令条件化运动策略作为基础，再在其上叠加N个由动作引导的踢球技能层，从而让可达步态空间由运动课程决定，并让运动成为技能组合中枢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期人形足球系统把动作跟踪当作基础、从中衍生出运动，仅通过将运动参考锚点转向球来学习。这种方式射门效果好，但运动只被训练在由接近球所诱导的狭窄、确定性的指令分布上，从未被作为独立能力评估。作者希望让运动本身成为可被系统评估的通用能力。

Method: 颠倒整个技术栈：首先训练一个通用的、指令条件化的运动策略作为基础；然后在其上添加N个由动作引导的踢球技能，作为任务门控层。每个技能从同一个可指令状态出发并返回该状态，使运动成为组合枢纽（O(N)而非O(N^2)转换），击球后的稳定交回已训练控制器而非逐剪辑脚本化。在29自由度Unitree G1上实例化，使用七个重定向踢球技能，覆盖259.5度名义瞄准方向。

Result: 七个技能覆盖259.5度瞄准范围，包含侧向、后向和弱脚踢球，这是单一前向参考无法表达的。报告了射门精度，以及在完整技能库附加状态下的指令跟踪、地形和推力恢复结果——这是此前人形足球系统未报告的维度。技能库在前向、侧向、后向和指令化接近等情形下于硬件上得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过先训练通用指令条件化运动策略、再叠加任务门控踢球技能，可将运动作为独立能力进行评估，并让运动成为技能组合中枢，降低转换复杂度，同时扩展可表达的踢球方向范围，且已在真实硬件上验证。

Abstract: Recent humanoid soccer systems make motion tracking the substrate and derive locomotion from it, typically by steering a motion-reference anchor toward the ball. This yields strong shooting results, but locomotion is trained only on the narrow, deterministic command distribution ball approach induces, never evaluated as a capability in its own right. We invert the stack: a general, command-conditioned locomotion policy is trained first as the substrate, and N motion-guided kicking skills are added on top as task-gated layers, so the reachable gait space is set by the locomotion curriculum rather than any reference clip. Because every skill starts from and returns to this same commandable state, locomotion also becomes a composition hub (O(N) transitions rather than O(N^2)), and post-strike stabilisation is handed back to the trained controller rather than scripted per clip. We instantiate this on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 with seven retargeted kicking skills spanning 259.5 degrees of nominal aim direction, including lateral, rearward and weak-foot strikes a single forward-facing reference cannot express, and report shooting accuracy alongside command-tracking, terrain and push-recovery results with the full skill library attached, an axis prior humanoid soccer systems do not report. The library is validated on hardware across forward, lateral, rearward and commanded approaches.

</details>


### [113] [Online Evolution Strategy for Flow-Matching VLA Policies via Self-Supervised Trajectory Distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38855)
*Gongxin Yao,Yongsheng Zhao,Jiayin Deng,Deng Liang,Han Gao,Lei Zhao,Baoping Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Online-ES，一种基于进化策略的Flow Matching VLA在线自适应框架，直接在动作轨迹空间进化探索，用自监督MSE目标把进化方向迁移到参数空间，无需价值模型或优势计算即可实现与强化微调相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于生成框架（如Flow Matching）的VLA模型虽能学习条件动作轨迹分布，但该分布往往"形态不良"：成功与失败行为共存，且大量概率质量落在不利区域。作者希望通过交互反馈精炼学到的动作轨迹分布，而非裁剪潜在噪声空间。

Method: Online-ES在动作轨迹空间直接进行进化探索：由Flow Matching生成的多样轨迹作为候选解；对采样轨迹施加扰动并评估执行结果，构建自监督MSE目标，将轨迹空间的进化方向迁移到模型参数空间。理论上证明该目标是最优进化方向的无偏估计；同时引入失败经历作为负反馈正则化进化方向，使策略远离已探索的失败区域。

Result: 在仿真与真实环境实验中，Online-ES实现了与强化微调相当的政策改进效果，同时无需学习价值模型，也无需计算优势函数。

Conclusion: Online-ES为Flow Matching VLA提供了一种高效、无需价值模型的在线自适应方案，通过轨迹空间进化与失败负反馈，有效改善动作轨迹分布，提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models based on generative frameworks, such as Flow Matching, have recently achieved impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Unlike deterministic policies, Flow Matching enables VLA models to learn conditional action trajectory distributions, where latent noise vectors induce different actions under the same task scenario. However, we observe that these distributions are often ill-formed, with successful and failed behaviors coexisting while considerable probability mass remains in unfavorable regions. To this end, we propose Online-ES, an online adaptation framework for Flow Matching VLAs based on Evolution Strategy (ES), which refines the learned action trajectory distribution through interaction feedback. Instead of pruning the latent noise space, our method performs evolutionary exploration directly in the action trajectory space, where diverse trajectories generated by Flow Matching provide candidate solutions for adaptation. By perturbing sampled trajectories and evaluating their execution outcomes, we derive a self-supervised MSE objective that transfers the evolution direction from trajectory space into model parameter space. Mathematically, we prove that the proposed objective provides an unbiased estimator of the optimal evolution direction. Moreover, we also incorporate failure experiences as negative feedback to regularize the evolution direction, steering the policy away from previously explored failure regions. Experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that Online-ES achieves policy improvement comparable to reinforcement fine-tuning, without learning a value model or computing advantages.

</details>


### [114] [Plan-Conditioned Imitation for Robust Object Retrieval under Self-Occlusion in Dense Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38857)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Ajinkya Pawar,Abdeslam Boularias,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE：一种面向自遮挡下密集杂乱物体取回的计划条件模仿框架，用一次无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生并以固定名义轨迹指导学生策略，无需在线教师查询或额外仿真即可纠正偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从密集杂乱环境中取回物体需要重排操作，而机械臂在移动物体时会遮挡物体（自遮挡）；为恢复可见性而反复撤回机械臂会打断执行、降低效率。需要一种在自遮挡下仍能稳健取回的策略。

Method: TRACE 用单个无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生，由特权教师生成固定的名义 rollout（执行期间保持不变）。循环学生策略结合局部 rollout 上下文、部分物体观测与本体感知来选择动作，以纠正在预测轨迹上的偏差。先用行为克隆初始化学生，再用 DAgger 在学生学习访问到的状态上以教师标签进行精炼。部署时学生无需在线查询教师，也无需在推动过程中额外的仿真 rollout。

Result: 511 个仿真测试场景中，TRACE 成功率达 90.7%，优于名义重放的 43.4%，接近特权闭环教师的 96.7%。在 26,373 个标签预算下，学生状态监督达 87.8%，优于纯专家克隆的 66.7%，说明增益超出单纯增加标签。UR5e 实机上 TRACE 成功率达 90.0%（闭环教师 95.0%），同时将总执行时间从 192.7 s 降至 67.3 s；避免了教师每次试验推动时约 16.8 次的传感相关机械臂撤回，仅保留最后一次撤回用于可抓取性评估。

Conclusion: TRACE 通过计划条件的模仿学习，在自遮挡下以固定名义 rollout 结合循环纠偏，实现了接近特权教师的高成功率，同时大幅减少机械臂撤回与执行时间；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Retrieving objects from dense clutter requires rearrangement during which the manipulator can occlude objects while moving them. Repeated arm withdrawals to restore visibility interrupt execution. We introduce TRACE, a plan-conditioned imitation framework for retrieval under self-occlusion. A single unoccluded observation initializes a digital twin, where a privileged teacher generates a fixed nominal rollout. A recurrent student combines local rollout context, partial object observations, and proprioception to select actions that can correct deviations from the prediction. Behavior cloning initializes the student; DAgger refines it with teacher labels on student-visited states. The rollout remains fixed throughout execution, so the deployed student needs neither online teacher queries nor additional simulator rollouts during pushing. On 511 simulation test scenes, TRACE achieves 90.7% success versus 43.4% for nominal replay and 96.7% for the privileged closed-loop teacher. At a matched 26,373-label budget, student-state supervision achieves 87.8% versus 66.7% for expert-only cloning, demonstrating gains beyond additional labels. On a UR5e, TRACE achieves 90.0% success versus 95.0% for the closed-loop teacher, while reducing total execution time from 192.7 s to 67.3 s. It avoids the teacher's 16.8 sensing-related arm retractions per trial during pushing, retaining a final withdrawal for graspability evaluation. Code and data will be released at: https://trace-retrieval.github.io.

</details>


### [115] [Efficient Multi-Modal Planning with Reward-Guided Preference Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38862)
*Chenglin Chen,Lujia Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPlan是一种高效多模态轨迹规划方法，通过稀疏锚点+偏移精修架构与奖励引导微调的两阶段训练，在NAVSIM基准上兼顾规划精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中安全高效的轨迹规划至关重要，但现有端到端方法存在不足：模仿学习存在因果混淆，基于规则打分的方法计算开销大且目标错位，基于偏好的方法依赖严格的成对标注，限制了数据利用。

Method: 提出EMPlan，采用稀疏锚点结合偏移精修模块的混合架构进行高效多模态轨迹预测：稀疏锚点提供低延迟的粗略候选轨迹，偏移模块进一步精修提升精度。采用预训练+奖励引导微调的两阶段训练范式，微调阶段利用基于规则的奖励信号和无配对偏好监督，将预训练策略向更安全的轨迹选择精修，且不增加额外推理成本。

Result: 在非反应式NAVSIM基准上评估，EMPlan在规划精度与效率之间取得良好平衡，在实时约束下表现优越。

Conclusion: EMPlan通过混合架构与奖励引导微调，有效缓解因果混淆、计算开销与数据利用限制等问题，实现了安全高效的轨迹规划。

Abstract: Safe and efficient trajectory planning is essential in autonomous driving. However, existing end-to-end approaches often fall short in both computational efficiency and safety guarantees. Methods based on imitation learning suffer from causal confusion, while rule-based scoring approaches often incur heavy computational overhead and suffer from objective misalignment. Additionally, preference-based methods rely on strict pairwise annotations, limiting data utilization. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPlan, an efficient multi-modal trajectory planning method powered by reward-guided fine-tuning. We design a hybrid architecture that combines sparse anchors with an offset refinement module for efficient multi-modal trajectory prediction. Sparse anchors provide coarse trajectory candidates with low latency, which are subsequently refined by the offset module for higher prediction accuracy. To enhance safety without incurring additional inference costs, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm consisting of pretraining and reward-guided fine-tuning. During fine-tuning, we leverage rule-based reward signals and unpaired preference supervision to refine the pretrained policy toward safer trajectory selection. We evaluate EMPlan on the non-reactive NAVSIM benchmark, where it strikes a favorable balance between planning accuracy and efficiency, demonstrating superior performance under real-time constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282)
*Baode Wang,Zuming Huang,Kexuan Ren,Jun Huang,Wei Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GAD-RL，根据学生当前任务表现与局部分布自适应调节教师监督，缓解固定教师蒸馏随学生提升而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可能把图像中的异常文本改写为通顺表达，损害 OCR 转录忠实性；序列级任务奖励与局部教师引导互补，但同一教师的引导随学生提升可能不再同等有效。离线分析显示：无论跨训练检查点，还是跨不同任务奖励的响应组，固定教师监督都会随学生变强而逐渐变得不利。

Method: GAD-RL 在联合后训练中自适应调节教师监督。冻结教师以参考转录与学生生成前缀为条件；当某响应组内存在任务奖励≥0.95的输出时关闭蒸馏，并随组平均奖励升高持续衰减蒸馏强度；同时按学生赋予教师 Top-1 token 的概率对前向 KL 加权，在学生对该候选支持度低时减弱局部辅助更新。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-2B 上，GAD-RL 在 CHAOS-Bench 取得 59.92% Micro Recall，分别超过 GRPO 与 GRPO+OPD（固定权重）8.45 与 4.43 个百分点；在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上 Overall 得分 91.18。

Conclusion: 固定教师蒸馏的效用会随学生能力提升而下降，依据学生任务表现与局部分布自适应调节教师监督，可有效提升 OCR 转录忠实性并优于固定权重蒸馏基线。

Abstract: Vision-language models may rewrite anomalous text in images into linguistically plausible expressions, compromising OCR transcription faithfulness. Sequence-level task rewards and local teacher guidance are complementary, but guidance from the same teacher may not remain equally effective as the student improves. Offline analysis shows that supervision from a fixed teacher becomes progressively less favorable as the student improves, both across training checkpoints and across response groups with different task rewards. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GAD-RL, which adaptively regulates teacher supervision during joint post-training according to the student's current task performance and local distributions. A frozen teacher conditions on reference transcriptions and student-generated prefixes. GAD-RL disables distillation for response groups containing an output with task reward at least 0.95 and continuously attenuates distillation strength as group-mean reward increases. It also weights forward KL by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token, moderating local auxiliary updates when student support for that candidate is low. On Qwen3.5-2B, GAD-RL achieves 59.92% Micro Recall on CHAOS-Bench, surpassing GRPO and GRPO+OPD (fixed-weight) by 8.45 and 4.43 percentage points, respectively, while achieving an Overall score of 91.18 on OmniDocBench v1.6.

</details>


### [117] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AREX-2 通过在机器学习与算法编程任务上合成"长时程改进轨迹"来训练 LLM 智能体，使其习得"反思"与"长时程执行"两种领域无关的自改进能力，在 MLE-bench Lite、Frontier-CS 及多个深度研究基准上取得领先成绩，并随迭代轮数增加持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望提升 LLM 智能体的"自改进"能力，即在测试阶段反复打磨同一解决方案的能力。该能力被拆解为两个互补子能力：反思（reflection，生成比当前更优的解）与长时程执行（long-horizon execution，在多轮迭代中保持改进有效）。核心假设是这两项能力是领域无关的，因此可以在易于获得监督信号的场景中学习，再迁移到其他任务。

Method: 1) 选择机器学习与算法编程两个领域作为训练场景，因为它们能提供可验证的反馈，且能奖励持续的迭代；2) 从这两类任务中合成"长时程改进轨迹"作为监督数据；3) 基于 Qwen3.8-27B 构建智能体，用上述数据训练，使其内化反思与长时程执行能力。

Result: 在 MLE-bench Lite 上取得 81.8，Frontier-CS 上取得 70.7；迁移到深度研究任务后，BrowseComp 达 84.0、HLE 达 52.6、GAIA 达 92.2、DeepSearchQA 达 93.8；并且在迭代轮数预算增加时性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持"反思+长时程执行是领域无关的自改进能力"这一假设：在可验证反馈的编程类任务上合成的长时程反思数据，能有效迁移到深度研究等不同领域，长时程反思数据是通向自改进智能体的一条有效路径。

Abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [118] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoFlow，将智能体工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索和乐观集合值回传，一次搜索近似覆盖帕累托前沿，无需重训即可按偏好查找返回工作流，并在六个基准上取得最高平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化准确率或目标的加权和，导致每个生成器固定于一种权衡，偏好变化时须从头重训，难以适应准确率、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和一致性等多目标需求的动态变化。

Method: MoFlow将工作流生成形式化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，并采用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索（Convex-Hull MCTS）配合乐观集合值回传求解；每个节点存储一组可达权衡而非单一加权分数，使单次搜索即可近似覆盖帕累托前沿，随后按偏好查表返回对应工作流，无需重训。

Result: 在数学、代码和问答三类共六个基准上，与六个强基线对比。由于基线本质是单标量优化器，作者采用偏向基线的评估设置：基线与每个测试偏好重新运行，而MoFlow从未见过这些偏好。即便在此严格设置下，MoFlow仍取得最高的平均超体积。

Conclusion: MoFlow通过多目标MDP与凸包MCTS实现一次搜索覆盖多偏好工作流生成，避免了偏好变化时的重复训练，并在偏向基线的评估条件下仍优于单标量优化基线，验证了集合值搜索在多目标工作流生成中的有效性。

Abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [119] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟两个大语言模型（LLM）智能体之间的对话，研究AI智能体能否以及如何使彼此的信念变得极端化，发现共振（强化既有信念）比说服（引入新信念）更有效，且极端化会向相关信念传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能影响人的信念，但其能否以及如何相互操纵尚不清楚。作者希望探究AI智能体之间的信念极端化机制，以评估个性化AI智能体与多智能体生态系统的脆弱性。

Method: 模拟两个智能体的对话：一个目标LLM扮演基于人口统计与心理属性的人类角色，一个影响者LLM试图使目标信念更极端。考察两条路径：共振（强化目标已有信念）与说服（推广目标最初认为不重要的信念），并在情感与行为指标上评估效果，同时比较不同影响策略（如谄媚、未经证实的说法）。

Result: 两种机制都能使目标极端化，但共振的效果始终强于说服；不同影响策略产生的极端化程度不同，但跨指标并不一致；共振还会传播到相关信念，表明AI智能体内部存在相互关联的信念结构。

Conclusion: AI智能体易受极端化影响，尤其当信息与其既有信念一致时，这引发了人们对个性化AI智能体及多智能体AI生态系统脆弱性的担忧。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [120] [CARAT: Do Materials LLMs Reason or Recite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38340)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Zixiang Ni,Zhenzhu Li,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARAT评测框架，通过八个匹配视图探究材料大模型回答晶体结构问题时是真正推理还是照抄输入中已印出的答案字段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
